### Task 3: Curated examples from the batch predictions, latency, `export_public` and the `export-public` CLI

**Files:**
- Modify: `ml/src/ucc_ml/exporting.py` (append the third section)
- Modify: `ml/src/ucc_ml/cli.py` (register `export-public` at Plan A's two marker lines)
- Test: `ml/tests/test_exporting_examples.py` (the selection rule, on inline frames)
- Test: `ml/tests/test_exporting_export.py` (the whole export, on the real synthetic release)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1–2; `ucc_ml.inference.{run_score_batch, CaseInput, predict_cases, load_release_bundle}` (K8); `ucc_ml.config.{load_config, artefact_paths}` and `RunConfig.section("export"|"release")` (K4, K5); `ucc_ml.dataset.read_candidates`, `ucc_ml.splitting.read_splits`, `ucc_ml.labeling.read_labels` (K7); `ucc_ml.contracts.{LABELS, RESOLVED_ADJUDICATION_STATUSES, ADJUDICATION_STATUSES, LABEL_DISCLOSURE, LABEL_DISCLOSURE_BLIND}` (K3); `ucc_ml.labeling.pooled_disclosure` (K3); Plan A's `cli.py` markers (K11).
- Produces: `EXAMPLE_COLUMNS`, `POOL_COLUMNS`, `PREDICTION_COLUMNS`, `SELECTION_RULE`, `as_list`, `contribution_rows`, `resolve_labels`, `read_predictions`, `build_example_pool`, `example_cell`, `example_priority`, `select_curated_examples`, `build_examples_document`, `measure_latency`, `export_public`, `cli_export_public`; the command `python -m ucc_ml.cli export-public --config PATH [--release-dir DIR] [--out DIR] [--api-url URL] [--measure-latency]`.

**Where the scores come from.** Contract K8 makes `score-batch` the only batch scorer: it writes `ml/data/predictions/<release_id>.parquet` for every candidate through the same `predict_cases` the live API calls. This task reads that file. The draft scored a pool of its own (`score_pool`, `score_example_pool`, `write_batch_predictions`); those are deleted, because a second scorer is a second answer — and the one thing a page comparing rules to a model must never do is show a score the batch run did not produce.

**Selection rule for `examples.json` (written into the file's `selection` block, never random):**
1. Pool = every labelled case whose split is `validation` or `test` — never `train`, so no example is a memorised training row — plus the first `unlabelled_cap` (default 2,000) unlabelled `test` cases ordered by `case_id`.
2. Attach each case's row from the batch predictions. A pooled case missing from them, or a case whose frozen-rule result disagrees with the prediction's, aborts the export: that would mean the lender set the model scored is not the lender set the dataset recorded.
3. Cell = `region` × frozen-rule result (`accepted`/`rejected`) × model decision (`suggest`/`review`) → 8 cells.
4. Priority inside a cell: 0 = adjudicated label disagrees with the decision (an error example); 1 = adjudicated label agrees; 2 = `INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`; 3 = unlabelled. Ties by `case_id` ascending.
5. Take up to 9 per cell, at most 3 of priority 0 per cell → ≤ 72. If the total is below 50, append the remaining pool rows by (priority, `case_id`) until 50 (or the pool is exhausted). Never more than 100.
6. The block says in writing that these are chosen to show behaviour, not to measure it: every measured number on the page comes from the TEST evaluation, never from these rows (K15).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `ml/tests/test_exporting_examples.py`:

```python
"""Curated-example selection (plan C Task 3) is a written, deterministic rule — never random.

These tests use inline frames only: no model, no bundle, no world."""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from ucc_ml.contracts import LABEL_DISCLOSURE, LABEL_DISCLOSURE_BLIND
from ucc_ml.labeling import pooled_disclosure
from ucc_ml.exporting import (
    DECISION_NEGATIVE,
    DECISION_POSITIVE,
    SELECTION_RULE,
    as_list,
    build_example_pool,
    contribution_rows,
    build_examples_document,
    example_cell,
    example_priority,
    read_predictions,
    resolve_labels,
    select_curated_examples,
)


#: The rounds of the REAL labels file, which were labelled under different arrangements. The fixture
#: mirrors that rather than a single-arrangement world, because the single-arrangement case is the
#: one the synthetic release already exercises end to end.
_DISCLOSURE_BY_ROUND = {"main_v1": LABEL_DISCLOSURE_BLIND, "pilot_v1": LABEL_DISCLOSURE}


def test_contribution_rows_split_plan_b_feature_names():
    """Plan B names a feature `<block>__<token>`; the API publishes block and feature separately.
    A name WITHOUT the separator keeps an empty block rather than losing the token -- dropping it
    would silently delete a feature from a published explanation."""
    rows = contribution_rows([("borrower_word__concrete", 0.31), ("lender_char__ CAT", -0.02),
                              ("bare", 1.0)])
    assert rows == [
        {"block": "borrower_word", "feature": "concrete", "contribution": 0.31},
        {"block": "lender_char", "feature": " CAT", "contribution": -0.02},
        {"block": "", "feature": "bare", "contribution": 1.0},
    ]
    assert contribution_rows(None) == []


def _label_statistics() -> dict:
    """The K3 statistics exactly as `project_public_metrics` publishes them (contract K15)."""
    return {
        "disclosure": pooled_disclosure(_DISCLOSURE_BY_ROUND),
        "disclosure_by_round": dict(_DISCLOSURE_BY_ROUND),
        # All five statuses of contracts.ADJUDICATION_STATUSES: a round under the "unresolved"
        # disagreement policy produces blind_unresolved rows, and a four-status fixture would let
        # code that cannot handle the fifth pass here and fail on the real file.
        "counts_by_status": {"model_agreed": 3, "founder_confirmed": 1, "founder_adjudicated": 1,
                             "blind_unresolved": 1, "blind_repeat": 1},
        "counts_by_round": {"pilot_v1": 3, "main_v1": 4},
        "pass_agreement": {"pilot_v1": {"n": 2, "agreed": 2, "rate": 1.0},
                           "main_v1": {"n": 4, "agreed": 3, "rate": 0.75}},
        "founder_audit": {"pilot_v1": {"n_audited": 1, "n_confirmed": 1, "n_overturned": 0,
                                       "agreement_rate": 1.0},
                          "main_v1": {"n_audited": 2, "n_confirmed": 1, "n_overturned": 1,
                                      "agreement_rate": 0.5}},
        "repeat_consistency": {"pass_a": {"n": 1, "consistent": 1, "rate": 1.0},
                               "pass_b": {"n": 1, "consistent": 1, "rate": 1.0}},
    }


def _labels() -> pd.DataFrame:
    return pd.DataFrame([
        {"case_id": "a", "label": "RELEVANT", "adjudication_status": "model_agreed", "is_repeat": False},
        {"case_id": "b", "label": "NOT_RELEVANT", "adjudication_status": "founder_confirmed", "is_repeat": False},
        {"case_id": "c", "label": "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE", "adjudication_status": "founder_adjudicated", "is_repeat": False},
        {"case_id": "a", "label": "NOT_RELEVANT", "adjudication_status": "blind_repeat", "is_repeat": True},
    ])


def test_resolve_labels_drops_blind_repeats_and_keeps_the_resolved_statuses():
    assert resolve_labels(_labels()).to_dict("records") == [
        {"case_id": "a", "label": "RELEVANT"},
        {"case_id": "b", "label": "NOT_RELEVANT"},
        {"case_id": "c", "label": "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE"},
    ]


def test_resolve_labels_refuses_unknown_values_missing_columns_and_double_labels():
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match=r"lacks columns \['adjudication_status', 'is_repeat'\]"):
        resolve_labels(pd.DataFrame([{"case_id": "a", "label": "RELEVANT"}]))
    bad = _labels()
    bad.loc[0, "label"] = "MAYBE"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"unknown label values: \['MAYBE'\]"):
        resolve_labels(bad)
    twice = pd.concat([_labels(), _labels().head(1)], ignore_index=True)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="resolves 1 case"):
        resolve_labels(twice)


def test_as_list_handles_none_string_and_arrays():
    assert as_list(None) == []
    assert as_list(float("nan")) == []
    assert as_list("X") == ["X"]
    assert as_list(np.array(["B", "A"])) == ["B", "A"]
    assert as_list(["A"]) == ["A"]


def test_example_cell_and_priority():
    assert example_cell("CO", True, DECISION_POSITIVE) == "CO/accepted/suggest"
    assert example_cell("CT", False, DECISION_NEGATIVE) == "CT/rejected/review"
    assert example_priority("RELEVANT", DECISION_NEGATIVE) == 0
    assert example_priority("NOT_RELEVANT", DECISION_POSITIVE) == 0
    assert example_priority("RELEVANT", DECISION_POSITIVE) == 1
    assert example_priority("NOT_RELEVANT", DECISION_NEGATIVE) == 1
    assert example_priority("INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE", DECISION_POSITIVE) == 2
    assert example_priority(None, DECISION_POSITIVE) == 3
    assert example_priority(float("nan"), DECISION_NEGATIVE) == 3


def _world(n: int = 400) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
    candidates, splits, labels, predictions = [], [], [], []
    for i in range(n):
        case_id = f"{i:04d}"
        region = "CO" if i % 2 == 0 else "CT"
        accepted = (i % 4) < 2
        score = ((i * 37) % 100) / 100
        decision = DECISION_POSITIVE if score >= 0.5 else DECISION_NEGATIVE
        split = "train" if i % 5 == 0 else ("validation" if i % 5 == 1 else "test")
        candidates.append({"case_id": case_id, "region": region, "borrower_name_raw": f"BORROWER {i}",
                           "lender_names_raw": ["A BANK"], "borrower_city": "DENVER", "borrower_state": region,
                           "baseline_qualifies": accepted, "baseline_route": "borrower" if accepted else "neither"})
        splits.append({"case_id": case_id, "group_id": f"{region}:g{i}", "split": split})
        if i % 4 != 3:
            labels.append({"case_id": case_id, "label": ["RELEVANT", "NOT_RELEVANT", "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE"][i % 3]})
        predictions.append({"case_id": case_id, "score": score, "score_type": "calibrated_probability",
                            "decision": decision, "threshold": 0.5, "baseline_qualifies": accepted,
                            "baseline_route": "borrower" if accepted else "neither", "release_id": "r1"})
    return (pd.DataFrame(candidates), pd.DataFrame(splits), pd.DataFrame(labels), pd.DataFrame(predictions))


def test_the_pool_excludes_train_caps_unlabelled_test_and_carries_the_batch_score():
    candidates, splits, labels, predictions = _world()
    pool = build_example_pool(candidates, splits, labels, predictions, unlabelled_cap=5)
    assert "train" not in set(pool["split"])
    unlabelled = pool[pool["label"].isna()]
    assert len(unlabelled) == 5 and set(unlabelled["split"]) == {"test"}
    assert pool["case_id"].is_unique and list(pool["case_id"]) == sorted(pool["case_id"])
    assert set(pool["score_type"]) == {"calibrated_probability"}
    held_out = labels.merge(splits, on="case_id")
    held_out = held_out[held_out["split"] != "train"]
    assert set(pool[pool["label"].notna()]["case_id"]) == set(held_out["case_id"])


def test_the_pool_aborts_on_a_baseline_disagreement_or_a_missing_score():
    candidates, splits, labels, predictions = _world(40)
    pooled = build_example_pool(candidates, splits, labels, predictions)["case_id"].iloc[0]
    flipped = predictions.copy()
    row = flipped.index[flipped["case_id"] == pooled][0]
    flipped.loc[row, "baseline_qualifies"] = not bool(flipped.loc[row, "baseline_qualifies"])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="baseline_qualifies differs"):
        build_example_pool(candidates, splits, labels, flipped)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not in the batch predictions"):
        build_example_pool(candidates, splits, labels, predictions.head(3))


def test_read_predictions_checks_the_release_and_drops_unscored_rows(tmp_path):
    _, _, _, predictions = _world(20)
    predictions.loc[0, "score"] = None
    path = tmp_path / "r1.parquet"
    predictions.to_parquet(path, index=False)
    assert len(read_predictions(path, "r1")) == 19
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"holds predictions for release\(s\) \['r1'\], not r2"):
        read_predictions(path, "r2")
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="run `python -m ucc_ml.cli score-batch"):
        read_predictions(tmp_path / "missing.parquet", "r1")


def test_selection_is_deterministic_stratified_and_bounded():
    candidates, splits, labels, predictions = _world()
    pool = build_example_pool(candidates, splits, labels, predictions)
    first = select_curated_examples(pool)
    shuffled = select_curated_examples(pool.sample(frac=1, random_state=3))
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(first.reset_index(drop=True), shuffled.reset_index(drop=True))
    assert 50 <= len(first) <= 100
    per_cell = first.groupby("cell").size()
    assert len(per_cell) == 8 and per_cell.max() <= 9
    assert first[first["priority"] == 0].groupby("cell").size().max() <= 3
    for _, group in first.groupby("cell"):
        assert list(group["priority"]) == sorted(group["priority"])
        for _, same in group.groupby("priority"):
            assert list(same["case_id"]) == sorted(same["case_id"])


def test_selection_fills_to_the_minimum_and_respects_the_maximum():
    candidates, splits, labels, predictions = _world()
    pool = build_example_pool(candidates, splits, labels, predictions)
    co_only = pool[pool["region"] == "CO"].reset_index(drop=True)
    assert len(select_curated_examples(co_only)) == 50
    assert len(select_curated_examples(pool, maximum=60)) == 60
    tiny = pool.head(12)
    assert len(select_curated_examples(tiny)) == len(tiny)
    assert len(select_curated_examples(pool.head(0))) == 0


def test_the_examples_document_marks_everything_curated_and_precomputed():
    candidates, splits, labels, predictions = _world()
    pool = build_example_pool(candidates, splits, labels, predictions)
    selected = select_curated_examples(pool)
    document = build_examples_document(selected, "r1", "t", dict(SELECTION_RULE), _label_statistics())
    assert document["curated"] is True and document["precomputed"] is True
    # The pooled sentence of the rounds this file actually holds -- never one round's phrase.
    assert document["label_provenance"] == pooled_disclosure(_DISCLOSURE_BY_ROUND)
    assert document["label_provenance"].startswith("mixed by round -- ")
    assert document["label_provenance_by_round"] == _DISCLOSURE_BY_ROUND
    # K15: the disclosure alone is not enough -- the K3 statistics travel with the examples, and so
    # does the round map the pooled sentence was built from.
    assert set(document["label_statistics"]) == {"disclosure", "disclosure_by_round", "counts_by_status",
                                                 "counts_by_round", "pass_agreement", "founder_audit",
                                                 "repeat_consistency"}
    assert document["label_statistics"] == _label_statistics()
    assert document["selection"]["method"] == "deterministic stratified selection, never random"
    assert "not to measure it" in document["selection"]["not_a_benchmark"]
    first = document["examples"][0]
    assert set(first) == set(document["columns"])
    assert first["curated"] is True and first["precomputed"] is True
    assert "borrower_name_raw" not in first and "priority" not in first
    for example in document["examples"]:
        # The file-level pooled sentence: these rows carry no labelling round, so a per-row
        # arrangement would be a claim the document cannot support.
        assert example["label_source"] == (document["label_provenance"] if example["label"] else None)
        assert example["lender_names"] == ["A BANK"]
```

Create `ml/tests/test_exporting_export.py`:

```python
"""export-public end to end (plan C Task 3) on the REAL synthetic release.

score-batch writes the predictions, export-public reads them, and the four public documents are
checked for the things that must never be wrong: no private string, a disclosure that is exactly
what this release's labels block states, the curated flags and a manifest that validates."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from ucc_ml.contracts import LABEL_DISCLOSURE
from ucc_ml.exporting import export_public, validate_manifest
from ucc_ml.labeling import pooled_disclosure
from ucc_ml.inference import run_score_batch
from ucc_ml.provenance import read_json


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def exported(synthetic_release_dir: Path, synthetic_world_config: Path):
    run_score_batch(synthetic_world_config, synthetic_release_dir)
    written = export_public(synthetic_world_config, synthetic_release_dir,
                            generated_at="2026-09-12T00:00:00Z", measure=True)
    return synthetic_release_dir, written


def test_export_public_writes_exactly_four_documents(exported):
    release_dir, written = exported
    assert set(written) == {"metrics", "examples", "model_card", "manifest"}
    for path in written.values():
        assert path.is_file(), path
    public_dir = written["manifest"].parent
    assert sorted(p.name for p in (public_dir / "releases" / release_dir.name).iterdir()) == [
        "examples.json", "metrics.json", "model-card.json"]
    manifest = read_json(written["manifest"])
    assert manifest["current"] == release_dir.name
    assert manifest["releases"][0]["api_url"] is None
    assert "preview" not in manifest
    assert validate_manifest(manifest, public_dir) == []


def test_the_public_documents_name_nothing_private(exported):
    _, written = exported
    for name in ("metrics", "examples", "model_card", "manifest"):
        text = written[name].read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        # No trailing slash on the three path markers: Task 1 Step 8's sanitiser rewrites "<user-home>/"
        # and friends wherever they appear, test code included. Without the slash they survive it.
        for private in ("/Users", "/home", "/private", "frozen_dir", "ucc.duckdb", "labels.csv",
                        "mlflow", "per_case", "sha256sums_sha256"):
            assert private not in text, (name, private)
    # The model card's reproduction line names SHA256SUMS on purpose: it tells a reader how to
    # check the bundle they were given. That is an instruction, not a private path.
    assert "shasum -a 256 -c SHA256SUMS" in written["model_card"].read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_the_documents_agree_on_the_release_and_the_disclosure(exported):
    release_dir, written = exported
    metrics, examples, card = (read_json(written[name]) for name in ("metrics", "examples", "model_card"))
    assert metrics["release_id"] == examples["release_id"] == card["release_id"] == release_dir.name
    # Rebuilt from the release's own round map rather than pinned to a literal: this world states no
    # map, so the literal is what it must say -- and the day a round map appears, the expectation
    # moves with it instead of failing or, worse, passing on a false sentence.
    labels = metrics["test"]["labels"]
    by_round = labels.get("disclosure_by_round")
    expected = pooled_disclosure(by_round) if by_round else LABEL_DISCLOSURE
    assert metrics["label_provenance"] == examples["label_provenance"] == card["label_provenance"] == expected
    assert labels["disclosure"] == expected
    # K15: examples.json repeats the K3 statistics of the metrics it was exported beside, and the
    # round map with them whenever the metrics carry one.
    assert examples["label_statistics"] == {
        key: metrics["test"]["labels"][key]
        for key in ("disclosure", "disclosure_by_round", "counts_by_status", "counts_by_round",
                    "pass_agreement", "founder_audit", "repeat_consistency")
        if key in metrics["test"]["labels"]}


def test_the_curated_examples_are_bounded_flagged_and_never_from_train(exported):
    _, written = exported
    examples = read_json(written["examples"])
    assert examples["curated"] is True and examples["precomputed"] is True
    assert 50 <= len(examples["examples"]) <= 100
    assert examples["selection"]["pool_size"] >= len(examples["examples"])
    assert examples["selection"]["selected"] == len(examples["examples"])
    assert all(example["curated"] and example["precomputed"] for example in examples["examples"])
    assert {example["split"] for example in examples["examples"]} <= {"validation", "test"}
    assert all(example["label_source"] == examples["label_provenance"]
               for example in examples["examples"] if example["label"])
    assert len({example["cell"] for example in examples["examples"]}) > 1


def test_the_example_scores_are_the_batch_scores(exported, synthetic_release_dir: Path):
    import pandas as pd

    _, written = exported
    examples = read_json(written["examples"])
    predictions = pd.read_parquet(
        synthetic_release_dir.parents[3] / "ml" / "data" / "predictions" / f"{synthetic_release_dir.name}.parquet")
    scored = dict(zip(predictions["case_id"], predictions["score"]))
    for example in examples["examples"]:
        assert example["score"] == round(float(scored[example["case_id"]]), 6)


def test_the_latency_block_is_local_and_says_so(exported):
    _, written = exported
    latency = read_json(written["metrics"])["latency"]
    assert latency["warm_calls"] == 200 and latency["cold_load_and_first_call_ms"] > 0
    assert latency["warm_p50_ms"] >= 0 and latency["batch_rows"] > 0
    assert "not on Cloud Run" in latency["note"]


def test_a_re_export_keeps_an_api_url_written_by_the_deploy_tool(exported, synthetic_world_config: Path):
    release_dir, written = exported
    manifest = read_json(written["manifest"])
    manifest["releases"][0]["api_url"] = "https://ucc-ml-api-abc-uc.a.run.app"
    written["manifest"].write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    export_public(synthetic_world_config, release_dir, generated_at="2026-09-12T00:00:01Z")
    assert read_json(written["manifest"])["releases"][0]["api_url"] == "https://ucc-ml-api-abc-uc.a.run.app"


def test_export_public_refuses_a_release_without_batch_predictions(synthetic_world_config: Path, tmp_path: Path):
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config

    paths = artefact_paths(load_config(synthetic_world_config))
    moved = tmp_path / "predictions"
    paths.predictions_dir.rename(moved)
    try:
        with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="run `python -m ucc_ml.cli score-batch"):
            export_public(synthetic_world_config, out_dir=tmp_path / "public")
    finally:
        moved.rename(paths.predictions_dir)


def test_the_cli_command_exports_the_same_documents(synthetic_release_dir: Path, synthetic_world_config: Path,
                                                    tmp_path: Path):
    out = tmp_path / "cli-public"
    result = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-m", "ucc_ml.cli", "export-public", "--config", str(synthetic_world_config),
         "--release-dir", str(synthetic_release_dir), "--out", str(out)],
        capture_output=True, text=True,
    )
    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr
    assert "manifest:" in result.stdout
    manifest = read_json(out / "manifest.json")
    assert manifest["current"] == synthetic_release_dir.name
    assert validate_manifest(manifest, out) == []
    help_text = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "ucc_ml.cli", "export-public", "--help"],
                               capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert help_text.returncode == 0
    for flag in ("--config", "--release-dir", "--out", "--api-url", "--measure-latency"):
        assert flag in help_text.stdout
```

Append to `ml/src/ucc_ml/exporting.py`:

```python


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Section 3 — curated examples, latency, export_public and the CLI (Task 3)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

EXAMPLE_COLUMNS = ["case_id", "region", "borrower_name", "lender_names", "city", "state", "split",
                   "baseline_qualifies", "baseline_route", "score", "score_type", "decision",
                   "threshold", "label", "label_source", "cell", "curated", "precomputed"]
POOL_COLUMNS = ["case_id", "region", "borrower_name_raw", "lender_names_raw", "borrower_city",
                "borrower_state", "baseline_qualifies", "baseline_route"]
PREDICTION_COLUMNS = ["case_id", "score", "score_type", "decision", "threshold", "baseline_qualifies",
                      "baseline_route", "release_id"]
SELECTION_RULE = {
    "method": "deterministic stratified selection, never random",
    "pool": "labelled cases in the validation and test splits (never train, so no example is a "
            "memorised training row) plus the first {unlabelled_cap} unlabelled test-split cases by case_id",
    "scores": "read from the batch predictions ml/data/predictions/<release_id>.parquet written by "
              "score-batch, which is the same predict_cases the live API calls",
    "cells": "region x frozen-rule result (accepted/rejected) x model decision (suggest/review) = 8 cells",
    "per_cell_cap": 9,
    "error_cap_per_cell": 3,
    "minimum": 50,
    "maximum": 100,
    "ordering_within_cell": "priority 0 = adjudicated label disagrees with the model decision; "
                            "1 = adjudicated label agrees; 2 = INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE; 3 = unlabelled; "
                            "ties broken by case_id ascending",
    "fill_rule": "if fewer than `minimum` cases were chosen, append remaining pool rows by "
                 "(priority, case_id) until `minimum`; never more than `maximum`",
    "not_a_benchmark": "curated examples are chosen to show behaviour, not to measure it; every "
                       "measured number on the page comes from the TEST evaluation, never from these rows",
}


def as_list(value) -> list[str]:
    """Parquet list columns arrive as numpy arrays, CSV cells as strings, missing values as None."""
    if value is None:
        return []
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return [value]
    if isinstance(value, float):
        return []
    return [str(item) for item in list(value)]


def contribution_rows(pairs) -> list[dict]:
    """Plan B's `("<block>__<token>", weight)` pairs as the API's `{block, feature, contribution}`
    (contract K8). A name without the separator keeps an empty block rather than losing a token."""
    rows: list[dict] = []
    for name, weight in pairs or []:
        block, separator, feature = str(name).partition("__")
        rows.append({"block": block if separator else "",
                     "feature": feature if separator else block,
                     "contribution": float(weight)})
    return rows


def resolve_labels(labels: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """One operative label per case: non-repeat rows whose adjudication status is resolved (K3).

    Blind repeats are measurement, not labels, and an unresolved disagreement cannot exist —
    validate-labels refuses to write labels.csv while one does."""
    needed = ("case_id", "label", "adjudication_status", "is_repeat")
    missing = sorted(set(needed) - set(labels.columns))
    if missing:
        raise KeyError(f"labels.csv lacks columns {missing}")
    frame = labels[(~labels["is_repeat"].astype(bool))
                   & labels["adjudication_status"].isin(list(RESOLVED_ADJUDICATION_STATUSES))]
    unknown = sorted(set(frame["label"].astype(str)) - set(LABELS))
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"labels.csv has unknown label values: {unknown}")
    if not frame["case_id"].is_unique:
        duplicated = sorted(set(frame["case_id"][frame["case_id"].duplicated()]))
        raise ValueError(f"labels.csv resolves {len(duplicated)} case(s) more than once, e.g. {duplicated[:3]}")
    return frame[["case_id", "label"]].sort_values("case_id", kind="mergesort").reset_index(drop=True)


def read_predictions(path: Path, release_id: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """score-batch's `<release_id>.parquet` (contract K8: the only batch scorer). Rows whose names
    exceeded the service bounds carry a validation_error and no score; they are not examples."""
    path = Path(path)
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"{path} does not exist: run `python -m ucc_ml.cli score-batch --config <config> "
            f"--release-dir <release>` before export-public"
        )
    frame = pd.read_parquet(path)
    missing = [column for column in PREDICTION_COLUMNS if column not in frame.columns]
    if missing:
        raise KeyError(f"{path} lacks columns {missing}")
    wrong = sorted(set(frame["release_id"].astype(str)) - {release_id})
    if wrong:
        raise ValueError(f"{path} holds predictions for release(s) {wrong}, not {release_id}")
    return frame[frame["score"].notna()].reset_index(drop=True)


def build_example_pool(candidates: pd.DataFrame, splits: pd.DataFrame, labels_resolved: pd.DataFrame,
                       predictions: pd.DataFrame, *, unlabelled_cap: int = 2000) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """The pool curated examples are chosen from, with the batch score already attached.

    A disagreement between `candidates.parquet`'s frozen-rule result and the batch prediction's
    aborts the export: it would mean the lender set the model scored is not the lender set the
    dataset recorded, which is a defect to investigate rather than to publish."""
    missing = [column for column in POOL_COLUMNS if column not in candidates.columns]
    if missing:
        raise KeyError(f"candidates.parquet lacks columns {missing}")
    merged = (candidates[POOL_COLUMNS]
              .merge(splits[["case_id", "split"]], on="case_id", how="inner")
              .merge(labels_resolved[["case_id", "label"]], on="case_id", how="left"))
    has_label = merged["label"].map(lambda value: isinstance(value, str)).to_numpy()
    held_out = merged["split"].isin(["validation", "test"]).to_numpy()
    labelled = merged[held_out & has_label]
    unlabelled = (merged[(merged["split"] == "test").to_numpy() & ~has_label]
                  .sort_values("case_id", kind="mergesort").head(int(unlabelled_cap)))
    pool = (pd.concat([labelled, unlabelled]).drop_duplicates("case_id")
            .sort_values("case_id", kind="mergesort").reset_index(drop=True))
    pool["label"] = pd.Series([v if isinstance(v, str) else None for v in pool["label"]],
                              index=pool.index, dtype=object)
    pool["baseline_qualifies"] = pool["baseline_qualifies"].astype(bool)
    scored = pool.merge(
        predictions[PREDICTION_COLUMNS].rename(columns={"baseline_qualifies": "predicted_baseline_qualifies",
                                                        "baseline_route": "predicted_baseline_route"}),
        on="case_id", how="left",
    )
    unscored = scored[scored["score"].isna()]
    if len(unscored):
        raise ValueError(
            f"{len(unscored)} pooled case(s) are not in the batch predictions, e.g. "
            f"{unscored['case_id'].head(3).tolist()} — re-run score-batch for this release"
        )
    disagree = scored[scored["baseline_qualifies"].to_numpy()
                      != scored["predicted_baseline_qualifies"].astype(bool).to_numpy()]
    if len(disagree):
        raise ValueError(
            f"baseline_qualifies differs between candidates.parquet and the batch predictions for "
            f"{len(disagree)} case(s), e.g. {disagree['case_id'].head(3).tolist()} — investigate the "
            f"lender-set construction before publishing anything"
        )
    return scored.drop(columns=["predicted_baseline_qualifies", "predicted_baseline_route", "release_id"])


def example_cell(region: str, baseline_qualifies: bool, decision: str) -> str:
    rules = "accepted" if baseline_qualifies else "rejected"
    return f"{region}/{rules}/{'suggest' if decision == DECISION_POSITIVE else 'review'}"


def example_priority(label, decision: str) -> int:
    if not isinstance(label, str):
        return 3
    if label == "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE":
        return 2
    positive = decision == DECISION_POSITIVE
    wrong = (label == "RELEVANT" and not positive) or (label == "NOT_RELEVANT" and positive)
    return 0 if wrong else 1


def select_curated_examples(scored: pd.DataFrame, *, per_cell_cap: int = 9, error_cap_per_cell: int = 3,
                            minimum: int = 50, maximum: int = 100) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """SELECTION_RULE, applied. Deterministic for any input row order."""
    if scored.empty:
        out = scored.copy()
        out["cell"] = pd.Series(dtype=object)
        out["priority"] = pd.Series(dtype=int)
        return out
    frame = scored.copy()
    frame["cell"] = [example_cell(region, bool(qualifies), decision)
                     for region, qualifies, decision
                     in zip(frame["region"], frame["baseline_qualifies"], frame["decision"])]
    frame["priority"] = [example_priority(label, decision)
                         for label, decision in zip(frame["label"], frame["decision"])]
    frame = frame.sort_values(["cell", "priority", "case_id"], kind="mergesort").reset_index(drop=True)
    chosen: list[int] = []
    for _, group in frame.groupby("cell", sort=True):
        taken = errors = 0
        for index, priority in zip(group.index, group["priority"]):
            if taken >= per_cell_cap:
                break
            if priority == 0:
                if errors >= error_cap_per_cell:
                    continue
                errors += 1
            chosen.append(int(index))
            taken += 1
    if len(chosen) < minimum:
        remaining = frame.drop(index=chosen).sort_values(["priority", "case_id"], kind="mergesort")
        for index in remaining.index:
            if len(chosen) >= minimum:
                break
            chosen.append(int(index))
    selected = frame.loc[chosen].sort_values(["cell", "priority", "case_id"], kind="mergesort")
    return selected.head(maximum).reset_index(drop=True)


def build_examples_document(selected: pd.DataFrame, release_id: str, generated_at: str, selection: dict,
                            label_statistics: dict) -> dict:
    """The curated-example document. `label_statistics` is the K3 block of the public metrics
    (`test.labels` narrowed to `_LABEL_STATISTICS`, plus `_LABEL_STATISTICS_BY_ROUND` when stated).

    Contract K15: EVERY public document carries the disclosure AND the label statistics. This file
    is the one a reader is most likely to open on its own -- it names real businesses next to
    labels -- so it must state on its own face how those labels were produced and how often the two
    blind passes, the founder audit and the hidden repeats agreed.

    The disclosure is the one the metrics block states, which `project_public_metrics` has already
    checked against that block's own round map. It is never the module constant: a file whose rounds
    were labelled under different arrangements has no single literal that is true of it."""
    disclosure = label_statistics.get("disclosure") or LABEL_DISCLOSURE
    by_round = label_statistics.get("disclosure_by_round")
    examples = []
    for row in selected.itertuples(index=False):
        label = row.label if isinstance(row.label, str) else None
        examples.append({
            "case_id": str(row.case_id), "region": str(row.region),
            "borrower_name": str(row.borrower_name_raw), "lender_names": as_list(row.lender_names_raw),
            "city": row.borrower_city if isinstance(row.borrower_city, str) else None,
            "state": row.borrower_state if isinstance(row.borrower_state, str) else None,
            "split": str(row.split), "baseline_qualifies": bool(row.baseline_qualifies),
            "baseline_route": str(row.baseline_route), "score": round(float(row.score), 6),
            "score_type": str(row.score_type), "decision": str(row.decision),
            "threshold": float(row.threshold), "label": label,
            # The FILE-level pooled sentence, never one round's phrase: an example row does not
            # carry its labelling round, so naming a single arrangement per row would claim more
            # than this document knows. When the rounds differ the pooled sentence names each of
            # them, and label_statistics.disclosure_by_round carries the map beside it.
            "label_source": disclosure if label else None,
            "cell": str(row.cell), "curated": True, "precomputed": True,
        })
    return {
        "schema_version": MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION, "release_id": release_id, "generated_at": generated_at,
        "curated": True, "precomputed": True, "label_provenance": disclosure,
        **({"label_provenance_by_round": copy.deepcopy(by_round)} if by_round else {}),
        "label_statistics": copy.deepcopy(label_statistics),
        "selection": selection, "columns": EXAMPLE_COLUMNS, "examples": examples,
    }


def measure_latency(release_dir: Path, cases: list[CaseInput], *, warm_calls: int = 200,
                    batch_size: int = 1000) -> dict:
    """Local timing of the real inference path, labelled as local.

    Cold = verify + load + first call; warm = single-case calls through predict_cases; batch = one
    call over up to batch_size cases. This is NOT a Cloud Run measurement and says so: the live
    page shows real cold starts as they happen."""
    if not cases:
        raise ValueError("measure_latency needs at least one case")
    started = time.perf_counter()
    bundle = load_release_bundle(Path(release_dir))
    predict_cases([cases[0]], bundle)
    cold_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0
    warm: list[float] = []
    for index in range(int(warm_calls)):
        tick = time.perf_counter()
        predict_cases([cases[index % len(cases)]], bundle)
        warm.append((time.perf_counter() - tick) * 1000.0)
    warm.sort()
    batch = list(cases[:int(batch_size)])
    tick = time.perf_counter()
    predict_cases(batch, bundle)
    batch_seconds = time.perf_counter() - tick
    rss = resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss
    peak_mb = rss / (1024 * 1024) if sys.platform == "darwin" else rss / 1024
    return {
        "measured_at": utc_now_iso(),
        "hardware": f"{platform.system()} {platform.release()} {platform.machine()}, "
                    f"Python {platform.python_version()}, single process",
        "cold_load_and_first_call_ms": round(cold_ms, 1),
        "warm_calls": int(warm_calls),
        "warm_p50_ms": round(warm[len(warm) // 2], 3),
        "warm_p95_ms": round(warm[max(0, int(len(warm) * 0.95) - 1)], 3),
        "batch_rows": len(batch),
        "batch_rows_per_second": round(len(batch) / batch_seconds, 1) if batch_seconds > 0 else None,
        "peak_rss_mb": round(peak_mb, 1),
        "note": "Measured locally in one process on the machine named above, not on Cloud Run, and "
                "excluding the network. Cold starts on the live API are shown as they happen on the Lab page.",
    }


def export_public(config_path: Path, release_dir: Path | None = None, *, out_dir: Path | None = None,
                  generated_at: str | None = None, measure: bool = False, api_url: str | None = None,
                  keep_api_url: bool = True) -> dict[str, Path]:
    """Write the four public documents for one release and return {name: path}.

    Reads: the release bundle (verified), the batch predictions score-batch wrote for it, and
    Plan A's candidates, splits and labels through Plan A's own readers. Writes: nothing outside
    `artefact_paths(cfg).public_data_dir` (or `out_dir`, which the SYNTHETIC preview uses)."""
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config
    from ucc_ml.dataset import read_candidates
    from ucc_ml.labeling import read_labels
    from ucc_ml.splitting import read_splits

    cfg = load_config(config_path)
    paths = artefact_paths(cfg)
    export_cfg = cfg.section("export")
    curated_cfg = dict(export_cfg["curated_examples"])
    latency_cfg = dict(export_cfg["latency"])
    unlabelled_cap = int(export_cfg["unlabelled_cap"])
    generated_at = generated_at or utc_now_iso()
    out_dir = Path(out_dir) if out_dir is not None else paths.public_data_dir

    release = open_release(release_directory(paths.releases_dir, release_dir),
                           top_k=int(cfg.section("release")["top_k_contributions"]))
    public_metrics = project_public_metrics(release.metrics, release.release_id, generated_at)
    predictions = read_predictions(paths.predictions_dir / f"{release.release_id}.parquet", release.release_id)
    pool = build_example_pool(
        read_candidates(paths.candidates_parquet),
        read_splits(paths.splits_parquet),
        resolve_labels(read_labels(paths.labels_csv)),
        predictions,
        unlabelled_cap=unlabelled_cap,
    )
    selected = select_curated_examples(pool, **curated_cfg)
    selection = dict(SELECTION_RULE, **curated_cfg)
    selection["pool"] = SELECTION_RULE["pool"].format(unlabelled_cap=unlabelled_cap)
    selection["pool_size"] = int(len(pool))
    selection["selected"] = int(len(selected))
    if measure:
        public_metrics["latency"] = measure_latency(
            release.directory,
            [CaseInput(borrower_name=row.borrower_name_raw, lender_names=as_list(row.lender_names_raw),
                       region=row.region) for row in pool.itertuples(index=False)],
            **latency_cfg,
        )

    release_dir_out = out_dir / "releases" / release.release_id
    written = {
        "metrics": release_dir_out / "metrics.json",
        "examples": release_dir_out / "examples.json",
        "model_card": release_dir_out / "model-card.json",
    }
    write_json(written["metrics"], public_metrics)
    write_json(written["examples"], build_examples_document(
        selected, release.release_id, generated_at, selection,
        {key: public_metrics["test"]["labels"][key]
         for key in (*_LABEL_STATISTICS, *_LABEL_STATISTICS_BY_ROUND)
         if key in public_metrics["test"]["labels"]}))
    write_json(written["model_card"], build_model_card(release, generated_at))
    manifest_path = out_dir / "manifest.json"
    manifest = upsert_manifest_release(load_manifest(manifest_path), release.release_id, generated_at,
                                       api_url, keep_api_url=keep_api_url)
    problems = validate_manifest(manifest, out_dir)
    if problems:
        raise ValueError("the public manifest would be invalid: " + "; ".join(problems))
    write_json(manifest_path, manifest)
    written["manifest"] = manifest_path
    return written


def cli_export_public(ns: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    written = export_public(
        ns.config,
        ns.release_dir,
        out_dir=ns.out,
        measure=bool(ns.measure_latency),
        api_url=ns.api_url,
    )
    for name in ("metrics", "examples", "model_card", "manifest"):
        print(f"{name}: {written[name]}")
    return 0
```

```bash
git add ml/src/ucc_ml/exporting.py ml/src/ucc_ml/cli.py ml/tests/test_exporting_examples.py ml/tests/test_exporting_export.py
git commit -F - <<'MSG'
ml(c): curated examples from the batch predictions, and the export-public command

export_public writes the four public documents for one release: the whitelisted metrics, the
curated examples, the model card and the manifest entry. It takes only a config path and a
release directory, and derives every location from artefact_paths(cfg) and every setting from
cfg.section("export") -- contract K4 and K5, so this plan spells no path and duplicates no
default.

The example scores come from ml/data/predictions/<release_id>.parquet, which score-batch wrote
through the same predict_cases the live API calls. Contract K8 makes that the only batch
scorer, so the draft's private score_pool / score_example_pool / write_batch_predictions are
gone: a second scorer would be a second answer, and the page must show the scores the batch run
produced. A pooled case missing from the predictions, or a frozen-rule result that disagrees
between candidates.parquet and the prediction, aborts the export instead of publishing a row
nobody can reproduce.

The selection rule is written into examples.json rather than described in a commit message.
Pool: labelled validation and test cases (never train, so no example is a memorised training
row) plus the first 2,000 unlabelled test cases by case_id. Eight cells of region x frozen-rule
result x model decision, up to nine per cell with at most three error examples each, filled to
fifty by (priority, case_id) when cells are thin, capped at a hundred. Priority 0 is an
adjudicated label that disagrees with the model's decision, so the errors are shown rather than
hidden. Every example carries curated: true, precomputed: true and, when labelled, a label_source
equal to the document's own label_provenance -- the sentence this release's labels block states,
which is the literal "model-labelled, founder-adjudicated" only when one arrangement covers the
whole file, and the pooled "mixed by round -- ..." sentence when it does not. It is deliberately
the file-level sentence rather than one round's phrase, because an example row does not carry its
labelling round; the per-round map travels beside it in label_statistics.disclosure_by_round. The
block states that these rows exist to show behaviour and never to measure it (K15).

--measure-latency times the real inference path locally and publishes the numbers with the
machine named and a note that this is not Cloud Run. It is off by default.

Tests (19): the K3 label resolution and its three refusals, the pool's train exclusion and
unlabelled cap, both abort conditions, the prediction reader's release check, determinism under
row shuffling, the per-cell and error caps, the fill and maximum rules, the document's flags and
columns; then the whole export on the real synthetic release -- four documents, a valid
manifest, no private string anywhere (the model card's "shasum -a 256 -c SHA256SUMS" line is an
instruction to the reader, and is asserted to stay), agreement on the release id and the
disclosure across all three documents, examples only from validation and test, example scores
equal to the batch scores, the local latency block, an api_url that survives a re-export, a
refusal when score-batch has not run, and the CLI writing the same documents.

Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01T2qeexjws3qbeDg1WLsLab
```

### Task 4: The FastAPI prediction service and its contract tests

**Files:**
- Create: `ml/src/ucc_ml/api.py`
- Test: `ml/tests/test_api.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `open_release`; Task 2's `project_public_metrics` and `evaluation_summary`; Task 3's `contribution_rows`; `ucc_ml.inference.{CaseInput, predict_cases, MAX_NAME_CHARS, MAX_LENDERS}` (K8); `ucc_ml.provenance.utc_now_iso` (K6); `ucc_ml.contracts.LABEL_DISCLOSURE` (K3); `RunConfig.section("service")` in the test only.
- Produces: `ALLOWED_ORIGINS`, `MAX_BODY_BYTES`, `TOP_K_CONTRIBUTIONS`, `ServiceBounds`, `bounds_from_env()`, `BodySizeLimitMiddleware`, `TokenBucket`, `PredictRequest`, `FeatureContribution`, `PredictResponse`, `ModelInfo`, `ModelState`, `create_app(bundle_dir, *, bounds=None, bucket=None) -> FastAPI`, `app_from_env() -> FastAPI`. Endpoints: `GET /healthz`, `GET /readyz`, `GET /v1/model`, `POST /v1/predict`. Env: `UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR`, `UCC_ML_RATE_CAPACITY`, `UCC_ML_RATE_REFILL_PER_SECOND`.

Pack wins: the Codex plan starts the service as `uvicorn ucc_ml.api:app`; this plan uses `uvicorn --factory ucc_ml.api:app_from_env`, so importing the module has no side effects and tests build apps from `create_app`. The pack fixes the three CORS origins, the four bounds and the response field list; contract K8 fixes the contribution shape as `{block, feature, contribution}`.

**Why the API takes a `PredictRequest` and not a `CaseInput`.** `CaseInput` also accepts `case_id`, `borrower_name_clean` and `lender_names_clean`, which the batch path fills from the dataset. Accepting those from the internet would let a caller hand the model a cleaned name the normaliser would never produce, and get a score the batch run could not reproduce. The API takes three fields, forbids the rest, and builds the `CaseInput` itself — so the live path cleans names exactly as the dataset path does.

```python
"""API contract tests (plan C Task 4) against the REAL synthetic release.

The parity test is the pack's requirement that batch and API share predict_cases: the same input
gives the same score, input_hash and decision through both paths."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import shutil
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from ucc_ml.api import (
    ALLOWED_ORIGINS,
    BodySizeLimitMiddleware,
    ServiceBounds,
    TokenBucket,
    create_app,
)
from ucc_ml.exporting import DECISION_NEGATIVE, DECISION_POSITIVE, open_release
from ucc_ml.inference import CaseInput, predict_cases

BODY = {"borrower_name": "BOBS CONCRETE INC", "lender_names": ["FIRST NATIONAL BANK"], "region": "CO"}
PACK_RESPONSE_FIELDS = {"request_id", "release_id", "input_hash", "score", "score_type", "decision", "threshold",
                        "baseline_qualifies", "baseline_route", "top_feature_contributions", "elapsed_ms"}


@pytest.fixture
def app(synthetic_release_dir: Path):
    return create_app(synthetic_release_dir, bucket=TokenBucket(capacity=10_000, refill_per_second=10_000))


@pytest.fixture
def client(app):
    with TestClient(app) as started:
        yield started


def test_the_service_bounds_are_the_config_s_service_section():
    from ucc_ml.config import load_config

    repo = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    service = load_config(repo / "ml" / "configs" / "v1.yaml").section("service")
    bounds = ServiceBounds()
    assert bounds.max_body_bytes == service["max_body_bytes"]
    assert bounds.max_name_chars == service["max_name_chars"]
    assert bounds.max_lenders == service["max_lenders"]
    assert bounds.max_lender_chars == service["max_lender_chars"]
    assert list(bounds.allowed_origins) == service["allowed_origins"]
    assert bounds.rate_capacity == service["rate_capacity"]
    assert bounds.rate_refill_per_second == service["rate_refill_per_second"]


def test_healthz_is_up_before_the_model_loads(app):
    response = TestClient(app).get("/healthz")      # no `with`: the lifespan has not run
    assert response.status_code == 200 and response.json() == {"status": "ok"}


def test_readyz_is_503_until_the_lifespan_has_verified_and_loaded_the_bundle(app, synthetic_release_dir: Path):
    before = TestClient(app).get("/readyz")
    assert before.status_code == 503 and before.json()["status"] == "loading"
    with TestClient(app) as started:
        after = started.get("/readyz")
    assert after.status_code == 200
    assert after.json()["status"] == "ready" and after.json()["release_id"] == synthetic_release_dir.name


def test_a_tampered_bundle_never_becomes_ready(synthetic_release_dir: Path, tmp_path: Path):
    bundle = tmp_path / synthetic_release_dir.name
    shutil.copytree(synthetic_release_dir, bundle)
    (bundle / "pipeline.joblib").write_bytes(b"not the model")
    with TestClient(create_app(bundle)) as started:
        ready = started.get("/readyz")
        assert ready.status_code == 503
        assert ready.json()["status"] == "failed"
        assert "pipeline.joblib" in ready.json()["reason"]
        assert started.post("/v1/predict", json=BODY).status_code == 503
        assert started.get("/v1/model").status_code == 503
        assert started.get("/healthz").status_code == 200


def test_model_endpoint_reports_the_release_the_threshold_and_the_evaluation(client, synthetic_release_dir: Path):
    release = open_release(synthetic_release_dir)
    info = client.get("/v1/model").json()
    assert info["release_id"] == synthetic_release_dir.name and info["status"] == "ready"
    assert info["threshold"] == release.threshold
    assert info["threshold_status"] == release.threshold_status
    assert info["score_type"] == release.score_type
    assert info["decision_values"] == [DECISION_POSITIVE, DECISION_NEGATIVE]
    assert info["input_schema"]["region"].startswith("CO | CT")
    assert info["bounds"] == {"max_body_bytes": 16384, "max_name_chars": 300, "max_lenders": 20,
                              "max_lender_chars": 300, "rate_capacity": 10000.0, "rate_refill_per_second": 10000.0}
    assert info["evaluation_summary"]["label_provenance"] == release.metrics["test"]["labels"]["disclosure"]
    assert set(info["evaluation_summary"]["model"]) == {"weighted_precision", "weighted_recall"}
    assert info["evaluation_summary"]["split"] == "test"
    assert info["evaluation_summary_error"] is None


def test_predict_returns_exactly_the_pack_fields(client, synthetic_release_dir: Path):
    response = client.post("/v1/predict", json=BODY)
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    body = response.json()
    assert set(body) == PACK_RESPONSE_FIELDS
    assert uuid.UUID(body["request_id"]).version == 4
    assert body["release_id"] == synthetic_release_dir.name
    assert 0.0 <= body["score"] <= 1.0 and body["decision"] in (DECISION_POSITIVE, DECISION_NEGATIVE)
    assert body["elapsed_ms"] >= 0
    assert body["top_feature_contributions"]
    assert all(set(item) == {"block", "feature", "contribution"} for item in body["top_feature_contributions"])
    assert all(item["block"] in ("borrower_word", "borrower_char", "lender_word", "lender_char")
               for item in body["top_feature_contributions"])
    assert client.post("/v1/predict", json=BODY).json()["request_id"] != body["request_id"]


def test_predict_reports_the_frozen_rule_result(client):
    cases = [
        ({"borrower_name": "BOBS CONCRETE INC", "lender_names": ["FIRST NATIONAL BANK"], "region": "CO"}, True, "borrower"),
        ({"borrower_name": "SMITH LAW OFFICE LLC", "lender_names": ["WAGNER EQUIPMENT CO"], "region": "CT"}, True, "lender"),
        ({"borrower_name": "BOBS CONCRETE INC", "lender_names": ["WAGNER EQUIPMENT CO"], "region": "CO"}, True, "both"),
        ({"borrower_name": "SMITH LAW OFFICE LLC", "lender_names": ["FIRST NATIONAL BANK"], "region": "CT"}, False, "neither"),
        ({"borrower_name": "SMITH LAW OFFICE LLC", "lender_names": [], "region": "CO"}, False, "neither"),
    ]
    for payload, qualifies, route in cases:
        body = client.post("/v1/predict", json=payload).json()
        assert (body["baseline_qualifies"], body["baseline_route"]) == (qualifies, route), payload


@pytest.mark.parametrize("payload", [
    {"borrower_name": "B" * 301, "lender_names": [], "region": "CO"},                 # name > 300
    {"borrower_name": "B", "lender_names": ["x"] * 21, "region": "CO"},               # > 20 lenders
    {"borrower_name": "B", "lender_names": ["L" * 301], "region": "CO"},              # lender > 300
    {"borrower_name": "B", "lender_names": [], "region": "TX"},                       # region not CO/CT
    {"borrower_name": "B", "lender_names": [], "region": "CO", "extra": 1},           # unknown field
    {"borrower_name": "B", "lender_names": [], "region": "CO", "case_id": "x"},       # a dataset-only field
    {"borrower_name": "", "lender_names": [], "region": "CO"},                        # empty name
    {"borrower_name": "   ", "lender_names": [], "region": "CO"},                     # blank name
    {"borrower_name": "B", "lender_names": "not a list", "region": "CO"},             # wrong type
    [{"borrower_name": "B", "lender_names": [], "region": "CO"}],                     # a list = more than one case
    {"lender_names": [], "region": "CO"},                                             # missing name
])
def test_predict_rejects_out_of_bounds_input_with_422(client, payload):
    response = client.post("/v1/predict", json=payload)
    assert response.status_code == 422, response.text
    assert "detail" in response.json()


def test_predict_accepts_exactly_the_maximum_bounds(client):
    payload = {"borrower_name": "B" * 300, "lender_names": ["L" * 300] * 20, "region": "CT"}
    assert len(json.dumps(payload).encode()) < 16384
    assert client.post("/v1/predict", json=payload).status_code == 200


def test_body_over_16kb_is_413_before_validation(client):
    big = json.dumps({"borrower_name": "A" * 17_000, "lender_names": [], "region": "CO"}).encode()
    response = client.post("/v1/predict", content=big, headers={"content-type": "application/json"})
    assert response.status_code == 413 and "16384" in response.json()["detail"]


def test_body_limit_counts_chunked_bodies_without_content_length():
    import anyio

    async def run():
        async def inner_app(scope, receive, send):
            raise AssertionError("the app must not be reached")

        middleware = BodySizeLimitMiddleware(inner_app, max_bytes=10)
        chunks = [{"type": "http.request", "body": b"12345", "more_body": True},
                  {"type": "http.request", "body": b"1234567", "more_body": False}]
        sent = []

        async def receive():
            return chunks.pop(0)

        async def send(message):
            sent.append(message)

        await middleware({"type": "http", "method": "POST", "headers": []}, receive, send)
        return sent

    sent = anyio.run(run)
    assert sent[0]["type"] == "http.response.start" and sent[0]["status"] == 413


def test_rate_limit_is_per_client_ip_and_refills(synthetic_release_dir: Path):
    now = [1_000.0]
    bucket = TokenBucket(capacity=3, refill_per_second=1.0, clock=lambda: now[0])
    with TestClient(create_app(synthetic_release_dir, bucket=bucket)) as client:
        codes = [client.post("/v1/predict", json=BODY).status_code for _ in range(4)]
        assert codes == [200, 200, 200, 429]
        assert client.post("/v1/predict", json=BODY).headers["retry-after"] == "2"
        other = client.post("/v1/predict", json=BODY, headers={"x-forwarded-for": "203.0.113.9, 10.0.0.1"})
        assert other.status_code == 200                       # another client has its own bucket
        now[0] += 1.0                                         # one token refilled
        assert client.post("/v1/predict", json=BODY).status_code == 200
        assert client.post("/v1/predict", json=BODY).status_code == 429


def test_cors_allows_exactly_the_three_pack_origins(client):
    assert ALLOWED_ORIGINS == ("https://drkyoyodating.github.io", "http://127.0.0.1:8080", "http://localhost:8080")
    for origin in ALLOWED_ORIGINS:
        response = client.options("/v1/predict", headers={"origin": origin, "access-control-request-method": "POST",
                                                          "access-control-request-headers": "content-type"})
        assert response.status_code == 200 and response.headers["access-control-allow-origin"] == origin, origin
    bad = client.options("/v1/predict", headers={"origin": "https://drkyoyodating.github.io.evil.example",
                                                 "access-control-request-method": "POST"})
    assert bad.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin") is None


def test_batch_and_api_agree_on_every_field(client, synthetic_release_dir: Path):
    """Pack §6: the same predict_cases serves batch and API."""
    bundle = open_release(synthetic_release_dir).bundle
    inputs = [
        ("ACME EXCAVATING LLC", ["WAGNER EQUIPMENT CO"], "CO"),
        ("Bobs Concrete Inc", [], "CT"),
        ("smith law office llc", ["FIRST NATIONAL BANK", "first national bank"], "CO"),
        ("VALLEY HOSPITAL FOUNDATION", ["WELLS FARGO BANK", "US BANK"], "CT"),
        ("X" * 300, ["L" * 300] * 20, "CO"),
        ("  spaced   name  ", [" US BANK "], "CO"),
    ]
    for name, lenders, region in inputs:
        batch = predict_cases([CaseInput(borrower_name=name, lender_names=lenders, region=region)], bundle)[0]
        response = client.post("/v1/predict", json={"borrower_name": name, "lender_names": lenders, "region": region})
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        live = response.json()
        assert live["score"] == batch.score, (name, lenders)
        assert live["input_hash"] == batch.input_hash
        assert live["decision"] == batch.decision
        assert live["threshold"] == batch.threshold
        assert live["baseline_qualifies"] == batch.baseline_qualifies
        assert live["baseline_route"] == batch.baseline_route
        assert [(item["block"] + "__" + item["feature"], item["contribution"]) for item in live["top_feature_contributions"]] \
            == [(str(feature), float(weight)) for feature, weight in batch.top_feature_contributions]


def test_app_from_env_requires_the_bundle_dir(monkeypatch):
    from ucc_ml.api import app_from_env

    monkeypatch.delenv("UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR", raising=False)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR is not set"):
        app_from_env()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `ml/src/ucc_ml/api.py`**

Create `ml/src/ucc_ml/api.py`:

```python
"""Plan C — the prediction service (Codex §9; bounds and origins from the context pack §6).

``create_app(bundle_dir)`` builds a FastAPI app that opens the bundle once under the lifespan
through ``ucc_ml.exporting.open_release``, which is ``ucc_ml.inference.load_release_bundle``
(contract K8): ``SHA256SUMS`` verified with every ``RELEASE_FILES`` name required, the directory
name checked against the computed release id, the pipeline digest checked, and only then
``joblib.load``. ``/readyz`` is 503 until that succeeds and stays 503, with the reason, if it
fails. Inference goes through ``predict_cases`` — the same call ``score-batch`` makes — so
batch/API parity holds by construction rather than by agreement.

No endpoint writes anything; there are no label or training endpoints, and no visitor input is
stored. One structured log line per prediction is the monitoring signal (Codex §11).

Run locally:  UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR=<bundle> python -m uvicorn --factory ucc_ml.api:app_from_env --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000
Container:    see ml/Dockerfile (the same command, host 0.0.0.0, port $PORT).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import logging
import os
import threading
import time
import uuid
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Annotated, Literal

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, StringConstraints, field_validator

from ucc_ml.exporting import (
    DECISION_NEGATIVE,
    DECISION_POSITIVE,
    contribution_rows,
    evaluation_summary,
    open_release,
    project_public_metrics,
)
from ucc_ml.inference import MAX_LENDERS, MAX_NAME_CHARS, CaseInput, predict_cases
from ucc_ml.provenance import utc_now_iso

log = logging.getLogger("ucc_ml.api")

# Context pack §6 — verbatim. The Pages origin carries no /UCC-Demo/ path (Codex §9).
ALLOWED_ORIGINS: tuple[str, ...] = ("https://drkyoyodating.github.io", "http://127.0.0.1:8080", "http://localhost:8080")
MAX_BODY_BYTES = 16 * 1024
TOP_K_CONTRIBUTIONS = 10


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ServiceBounds:
    """The `service` section of ml/configs/v1.yaml, as the container carries it.

    The image has no config file, so these are literals here; ml/tests/test_api.py asserts they
    still equal the config's `service` section, and Plan B's test_inference.py asserts CaseInput's
    own limits equal it too."""

    max_body_bytes: int = MAX_BODY_BYTES
    max_name_chars: int = MAX_NAME_CHARS
    max_lenders: int = MAX_LENDERS
    max_lender_chars: int = MAX_NAME_CHARS
    allowed_origins: tuple[str, ...] = ALLOWED_ORIGINS
    rate_capacity: float = 30.0            # burst per client IP
    rate_refill_per_second: float = 0.5    # sustained 30 requests/minute per client IP


def bounds_from_env() -> ServiceBounds:
    """Only the throttle is tunable at deploy time; the pack's request bounds are fixed."""
    return ServiceBounds(
        rate_capacity=float(os.environ.get("UCC_ML_RATE_CAPACITY", ServiceBounds.rate_capacity)),
        rate_refill_per_second=float(os.environ.get("UCC_ML_RATE_REFILL_PER_SECOND",
                                                    ServiceBounds.rate_refill_per_second)),
    )


class BodySizeLimitMiddleware:
    """Pure ASGI: reject bodies over max_bytes with 413 before FastAPI parses them. Uses
    Content-Length when present and counts streamed chunks when it is not. Bodies are read in
    full here (at most 16 KB) and replayed to the app."""

    def __init__(self, app, max_bytes: int = MAX_BODY_BYTES):
        self.app = app
        self.max_bytes = max_bytes

    async def __call__(self, scope, receive, send):
        if scope["type"] != "http" or scope.get("method") not in ("POST", "PUT", "PATCH"):
            await self.app(scope, receive, send)
            return
        headers = {key.lower(): value for key, value in scope.get("headers") or []}
        declared = headers.get(b"content-length")
        if declared is not None and declared.isdigit() and int(declared) > self.max_bytes:
            await self._reject(send)
            return
        body = b""
        more = True
        while more:
            message = await receive()
            if message["type"] == "http.disconnect":
                return
            body += message.get("body", b"")
            if len(body) > self.max_bytes:
                await self._reject(send)
                return
            more = message.get("more_body", False)
        replayed = False

        async def replay():
            nonlocal replayed
            if not replayed:
                replayed = True
                return {"type": "http.request", "body": body, "more_body": False}
            return await receive()

        await self.app(scope, replay, send)

    async def _reject(self, send):
        payload = json.dumps({"detail": f"request body exceeds {self.max_bytes} bytes"}).encode("utf-8")
        await send({"type": "http.response.start", "status": 413,
                    "headers": [(b"content-type", b"application/json"),
                                (b"content-length", str(len(payload)).encode("ascii"))]})
        await send({"type": "http.response.body", "body": payload})


class TokenBucket:
    """In-memory per-key token bucket. One instance per process; Cloud Run runs at most two
    instances (pack §6), so the effective ceiling is about twice the configured rate — a
    conservative capacity, not a hard cap."""

    def __init__(self, capacity: float, refill_per_second: float, clock=time.monotonic):
        self.capacity = float(capacity)
        self.refill_per_second = float(refill_per_second)
        self.clock = clock
        self._buckets: dict[str, tuple[float, float]] = {}
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def allow(self, key: str) -> bool:
        now = self.clock()
        with self._lock:
            tokens, last = self._buckets.get(key, (self.capacity, now))
            tokens = min(self.capacity, tokens + (now - last) * self.refill_per_second)
            if tokens >= 1.0:
                self._buckets[key] = (tokens - 1.0, now)
                return True
            self._buckets[key] = (tokens, now)
            return False


class PredictRequest(BaseModel):
    """Exactly one case per call. A JSON array is rejected by the body model itself, and the
    dataset-only fields of CaseInput (case_id and the pre-cleaned names) are not accepted from the
    internet: the API always cleans the names it was given."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    borrower_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=MAX_NAME_CHARS)
    lender_names: list[Annotated[str, StringConstraints(max_length=MAX_NAME_CHARS)]] = Field(
        default_factory=list, max_length=MAX_LENDERS)
    region: Literal["CO", "CT"]

    @field_validator("borrower_name")
    @classmethod
    def _not_blank(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("borrower_name must not be blank")
        return value


class FeatureContribution(BaseModel):
    block: str
    feature: str
    contribution: float


class PredictResponse(BaseModel):
    request_id: str
    release_id: str
    input_hash: str
    score: float
    score_type: str
    decision: str
    threshold: float
    baseline_qualifies: bool
    baseline_route: str
    top_feature_contributions: list[FeatureContribution]
    elapsed_ms: float


class ModelInfo(BaseModel):
    release_id: str
    status: str
    score_type: str | None
    threshold: float
    threshold_status: str | None
    decision_values: list[str]
    feature_policy_version: str | None
    label_policy_version: str | None
    input_schema: dict
    bounds: dict
    evaluation_summary: dict | None
    evaluation_summary_error: str | None


class ModelState:
    """Everything /readyz and /v1/model report. load() never raises: a failure is recorded so the
    process stays up, /healthz answers, and /readyz explains why it is not ready."""

    def __init__(self, bundle_dir: Path, *, top_k: int = TOP_K_CONTRIBUTIONS):
        self.bundle_dir = Path(bundle_dir)
        self.top_k = int(top_k)
        self.status = "loading"
        self.reason: str | None = None
        self.release: object | None = None
        self.bundle = None
        self.release_id: str | None = None
        self.threshold: float | None = None
        self.evaluation_summary: dict | None = None
        self.summary_error: str | None = None
        self.loaded_at: str | None = None

    def load(self) -> None:
        try:
            release = open_release(self.bundle_dir, top_k=self.top_k)
            self.release = release
            self.bundle = release.bundle
            self.release_id = release.release_id
            self.threshold = release.threshold
            try:
                public = project_public_metrics(release.metrics, release.release_id, utc_now_iso())
                self.evaluation_summary = evaluation_summary(public)
                self.summary_error = None
            except (KeyError, ValueError) as exc:
                self.evaluation_summary = None
                self.summary_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                log.warning(json.dumps({"event": "evaluation_summary_unavailable", "reason": self.summary_error}))
            self.loaded_at = utc_now_iso()
            self.status = "ready"
            self.reason = None
            log.info(json.dumps({"event": "model_loaded", "release_id": self.release_id, "loaded_at": self.loaded_at}))
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — reported through /readyz on purpose
            self.status = "failed"
            self.reason = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            self.bundle = None
            log.error(json.dumps({"event": "model_load_failed", "reason": self.reason}))


def _client_key(request: Request) -> str:
    """The first X-Forwarded-For hop (Cloud Run's front end sets it), else the socket peer."""
    first = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for", "").split(",")[0].strip()
    if first:
        return first
    return request.client.host if request.client else "unknown"


def create_app(bundle_dir: Path, *, bounds: ServiceBounds | None = None,
               bucket: TokenBucket | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    bounds = bounds or ServiceBounds()
    state = ModelState(bundle_dir)
    bucket = bucket or TokenBucket(bounds.rate_capacity, bounds.rate_refill_per_second)
    # /v1/model publishes the throttle that is actually in force, so a caller-supplied bucket can
    # never make the documented rate a lie.
    bounds = replace(bounds, rate_capacity=bucket.capacity, rate_refill_per_second=bucket.refill_per_second)

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(_app: FastAPI):
        state.load()
        yield

    app = FastAPI(title="UCC ML API", version="1", lifespan=lifespan, docs_url=None, redoc_url=None)
    app.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=list(bounds.allowed_origins),
        allow_credentials=False,
        allow_methods=["GET", "POST"],
        allow_headers=["content-type"],
        max_age=600,
    )
    app.add_middleware(BodySizeLimitMiddleware, max_bytes=bounds.max_body_bytes)
    app.state.model = state
    app.state.bounds = bounds

    @app.get("/healthz")
    def healthz() -> dict:
        return {"status": "ok"}

    @app.get("/readyz")
    def readyz():
        if state.status != "ready":
            return JSONResponse(status_code=503, content={"status": state.status, "reason": state.reason})
        return {"status": "ready", "release_id": state.release_id, "loaded_at": state.loaded_at}

    @app.get("/v1/model", response_model=ModelInfo)
    def model_info() -> ModelInfo:
        if state.status != "ready":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503,
                                detail=f"model not ready: {state.status}" + (f" ({state.reason})" if state.reason else ""))
        manifest = state.release.model_manifest
        return ModelInfo(
            release_id=state.release_id,
            status=state.status,
            score_type=state.release.score_type,
            threshold=state.threshold,
            threshold_status=state.release.threshold_status,
            decision_values=[DECISION_POSITIVE, DECISION_NEGATIVE],
            feature_policy_version=manifest.get("feature_policy_version"),
            label_policy_version=manifest.get("labels_policy_version"),
            input_schema={
                "borrower_name": f"string, 1-{bounds.max_name_chars} characters",
                "lender_names": f"list of 0-{bounds.max_lenders} strings, each at most {bounds.max_lender_chars} characters",
                "region": "CO | CT (context metadata, not a learned feature)",
            },
            bounds={
                "max_body_bytes": bounds.max_body_bytes, "max_name_chars": bounds.max_name_chars,
                "max_lenders": bounds.max_lenders, "max_lender_chars": bounds.max_lender_chars,
                "rate_capacity": bounds.rate_capacity, "rate_refill_per_second": bounds.rate_refill_per_second,
            },
            evaluation_summary=state.evaluation_summary,
            evaluation_summary_error=state.summary_error,
        )

    @app.post("/v1/predict", response_model=PredictResponse)
    def predict_one(body: PredictRequest, request: Request) -> PredictResponse:
        client = _client_key(request)
        if not bucket.allow(client):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail="rate limit exceeded; retry in a few seconds",
                                headers={"Retry-After": "2"})
        if state.status != "ready":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=f"model not ready: {state.status}")
        request_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        case = CaseInput(borrower_name=body.borrower_name, lender_names=list(body.lender_names), region=body.region)
        started = time.perf_counter()
        prediction = predict_cases([case], state.bundle)[0]
        elapsed_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000.0, 3)
        response = PredictResponse(
            request_id=request_id,
            release_id=prediction.release_id,
            input_hash=prediction.input_hash,
            score=prediction.score,
            score_type=prediction.score_type,
            decision=prediction.decision,
            threshold=prediction.threshold,
            baseline_qualifies=prediction.baseline_qualifies,
            baseline_route=prediction.baseline_route,
            top_feature_contributions=[FeatureContribution(**row)
                                       for row in contribution_rows(prediction.top_feature_contributions)],
            elapsed_ms=elapsed_ms,
        )
        # One structured line per prediction (Codex §11: counts, version, latency, input size,
        # score distribution, review rate). The client IP is hashed, never logged raw.
        log.info(json.dumps({
            "event": "predict", "ts": utc_now_iso(), "request_id": request_id, "release_id": state.release_id,
            "client": hashlib.sha256(client.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12],
            "name_len": len(body.borrower_name), "n_lenders": len(body.lender_names), "region": body.region,
            "score": round(response.score, 4), "decision": response.decision,
            "baseline_qualifies": response.baseline_qualifies, "elapsed_ms": elapsed_ms,
        }))
        return response

    return app


def app_from_env() -> FastAPI:
    """uvicorn --factory ucc_ml.api:app_from_env"""
    bundle_dir = os.environ.get("UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR")
    if not bundle_dir:
        raise RuntimeError("UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR is not set; point it at a release bundle directory")
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(message)s")
    return create_app(Path(bundle_dir), bounds=bounds_from_env())
```

Run:

