### Task 6: Dagster assets (`ucc_ml.definitions`) that are the same runs as the shell

**Files:**
- Create: `ml/src/ucc_ml/definitions.py`
- Test: `ml/tests/test_definitions.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - K11 `ucc_ml.cli.main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int` and these commands (all with `--config PATH`): A's `build-candidates`, `make-pilot`, `freeze-splits`, `make-main-round`, `label-blind --round {pilot_v1|main_v1} [--chunk-size N]` (K13.5), `validate-labels`; B's `train`, `freeze-candidate`, `evaluate-final`, `build-release`, `score-batch --release-dir DIR`; C Task 3's `export-public --release-dir DIR`.
  - K4/K5 `ucc_ml.config.load_config`, `artefact_paths(cfg)` fields `snapshot_manifest, candidates_parquet, splits_parquet, labels_csv, frozen_dir, releases_dir, predictions_dir, public_data_dir`.
  - K7 `ucc_ml.dataset.read_candidates(path)`, `ucc_ml.splitting.read_splits(path)`, `ucc_ml.labeling.read_labels(path)`; K3 `ucc_ml.contracts.RESOLVED_ADJUDICATION_STATUSES`.
  - K6 `ucc_ml.provenance.sha256_file`, `read_json`, `verify_sha256sums(directory, required=...)`; K8 `ucc_ml.inference.compute_release_id(directory) -> str`, `load_release_bundle(directory)`.
  - K10 `ucc_ml.synthetic.make_world`, `write_world(world, root)`, `write_config(root) -> Path` (test only).
  - B's frozen candidate files `threshold.json` (keys `threshold`, `status`) and `model-manifest.json`, copied byte for byte into the release by `build-release`.
- Produces:
  - `defs: dagster.Definitions` with 15 assets named as in Codex §11, `ASSETS: list[AssetsDefinition]`, resource key `ml_config`.
  - `MlConfigResource(config_path: str = "ml/configs/v1.yaml")` (`UCC_ML_CONFIG` overrides the default in `defs`), `ManualRunConfig(confirm_manual_run: bool = False)`, `LabelQueueConfig(labelling_round: str = "pilot_v1", chunk_size: int = 200)`, `ReleaseConfig(release_dir: str = "")`.
  - `_run_cli(argv: Sequence[str]) -> int`, `run_cli_step(argv: Sequence[str]) -> str` (raises `dagster.Failure` on a non-zero exit), `require_manual_confirmation(asset_name: str, config: ManualRunConfig) -> None`, `label_counts_by_split(splits, labels) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]`, `current_release_dir(config_path: Path) -> Path`.

Asset map (Codex §11; each `→` is a `deps=` edge, so no IO manager ever stores data):

| Asset | Does | Parents |
|---|---|---|
| `source_snapshot_manifest` | checks `artefact_paths.snapshot_manifest` (written under `.venv`, never here) | — |
| `candidate_cases` | `build-candidates` | source_snapshot_manifest |
| `grouping_and_sampling_manifest` | `make-pilot` | candidate_cases |
| `split_manifest` | `freeze-splits` (pilot groups go to train, K12) | grouping_and_sampling_manifest |
| `labeling_queue` | `label-blind --round R` (main_v1: `make-main-round` first, `--chunk-size`) | grouping_and_sampling_manifest, split_manifest |
| `approved_labels` | `validate-labels` (refuses while a disagreement is undecided) | labeling_queue |
| `training_dataset` | reads candidates/splits/labels via K7, TRAIN and VALIDATION counts | approved_labels, split_manifest |
| `trained_candidate` | `train` | training_dataset |
| `validation_report` | `freeze-candidate` | trained_candidate |
| `frozen_candidate_manifest` | verifies the frozen SHA256SUMS, records threshold/status | validation_report |
| `final_test_labels` | TEST resolved-label counts; never scores TEST | approved_labels, split_manifest |
| `final_evaluation_report` | MANUAL `evaluate-final` | frozen_candidate_manifest, final_test_labels |
| `release_bundle` | MANUAL `build-release` | final_evaluation_report |
| `batch_predictions` | `score-batch --release-dir` | release_bundle |
| `public_exports` | `export-public --release-dir` | batch_predictions |

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `ml/tests/test_definitions.py`:
```python
"""Dagster assets (plan C Task 6): the Codex section 11 graph, the CLI argv of every command asset,
the manual guard on the two TEST-touching assets, and one in-process run of the model half on the
synthetic world whose commands are exactly the shell commands."""
from __future__ import annotations

import dagster as dg
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from ucc_ml import definitions as d

CFG = "ml/configs/v1.yaml"
EXPECTED_PARENTS = {
    "source_snapshot_manifest": set(),
    "candidate_cases": {"source_snapshot_manifest"},
    "grouping_and_sampling_manifest": {"candidate_cases"},
    "split_manifest": {"grouping_and_sampling_manifest"},
    "labeling_queue": {"grouping_and_sampling_manifest", "split_manifest"},
    "approved_labels": {"labeling_queue"},
    "training_dataset": {"approved_labels", "split_manifest"},
    "trained_candidate": {"training_dataset"},
    "validation_report": {"trained_candidate"},
    "frozen_candidate_manifest": {"validation_report"},
    "final_test_labels": {"approved_labels", "split_manifest"},
    "final_evaluation_report": {"frozen_candidate_manifest", "final_test_labels"},
    "release_bundle": {"final_evaluation_report"},
    "batch_predictions": {"release_bundle"},
    "public_exports": {"batch_predictions"},
}
MODEL_HALF = ["training_dataset", "trained_candidate", "validation_report", "frozen_candidate_manifest",
              "final_test_labels", "final_evaluation_report", "release_bundle", "batch_predictions", "public_exports"]


def _materialize(selection, *, run_config=None, config_path=CFG):
    return dg.materialize(d.ASSETS, selection=selection, run_config=run_config, raise_on_error=False,
                          resources={"ml_config": d.MlConfigResource(config_path=config_path)})


def _failures(result) -> list[str]:
    return [e.step_failure_data.error.message for e in result.all_events if e.event_type_value == "STEP_FAILURE"]


def _confirm(*names: str) -> dg.RunConfig:
    return dg.RunConfig(ops={name: d.ManualRunConfig(confirm_manual_run=True) for name in names})


@pytest.fixture
def recorded(monkeypatch):
    calls: list[list[str]] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(d, "_run_cli", lambda argv: calls.append(list(argv)) or 0)
    return calls


def test_asset_graph_is_codex_section_11():
    graph = d.defs.resolve_asset_graph()
    assert {key.to_user_string() for key in graph.get_all_asset_keys()} == set(EXPECTED_PARENTS)
    for name, parents in EXPECTED_PARENTS.items():
        assert {key.to_user_string() for key in graph.get(dg.AssetKey(name)).parent_keys} == parents, name
    assert not any(isinstance(value, (dg.ScheduleDefinition, dg.SensorDefinition)) for value in vars(d).values())


def test_each_command_asset_runs_exactly_the_shell_command(recorded):
    expected = {
        "candidate_cases": [["build-candidates", "--config", CFG]],
        "grouping_and_sampling_manifest": [["make-pilot", "--config", CFG]],
        "split_manifest": [["freeze-splits", "--config", CFG]],
        "approved_labels": [["validate-labels", "--config", CFG]],
        "trained_candidate": [["train", "--config", CFG]],
        "validation_report": [["freeze-candidate", "--config", CFG]],
    }
    for name, argvs in expected.items():
        recorded.clear()
        result = _materialize([name])
        assert result.success, (name, _failures(result))
        assert recorded == argvs, name


def test_labeling_queue_runs_one_round(recorded):
    assert _materialize(["labeling_queue"]).success
    assert recorded == [["label-blind", "--config", CFG, "--round", "pilot_v1"]]
    recorded.clear()
    main_round = dg.RunConfig(ops={"labeling_queue": d.LabelQueueConfig(labelling_round="main_v1", chunk_size=200)})
    assert _materialize(["labeling_queue"], run_config=main_round).success
    assert recorded == [["make-main-round", "--config", CFG],
                        ["label-blind", "--config", CFG, "--round", "main_v1", "--chunk-size", "200"]]
    recorded.clear()
    unknown = dg.RunConfig(ops={"labeling_queue": d.LabelQueueConfig(labelling_round="final")})
    assert _materialize(["labeling_queue"], run_config=unknown).success is False
    assert recorded == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["final_evaluation_report", "release_bundle"])
def test_manual_assets_refuse_without_confirmation(recorded, name):
    result = _materialize([name])
    assert result.success is False
    assert recorded == []
    assert any("confirm_manual_run: true" in message for message in _failures(result))


def test_confirmed_final_evaluation_runs_evaluate_final(recorded):
    assert _materialize(["final_evaluation_report"], run_config=_confirm("final_evaluation_report")).success
    assert recorded == [["evaluate-final", "--config", CFG]]


def test_a_failing_command_fails_its_asset_with_the_exit_code(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(d, "_run_cli", lambda argv: 2)
    result = _materialize(["trained_candidate"])
    assert result.success is False
    assert any("`python -m ucc_ml.cli train --config ml/configs/v1.yaml` exited 2" in m for m in _failures(result))


def test_run_cli_is_the_cli_entry_point(capsys):
    assert d._run_cli(["--version"]) == 0
    assert "ucc_ml" in capsys.readouterr().out
    assert d._run_cli(["no-such-command"]) == 2


def test_label_counts_by_split_uses_only_resolved_non_repeat_rows():
    splits = pd.DataFrame({"case_id": list("abcde"), "group_id": ["CO:g"] * 5,
                           "split": ["train", "train", "validation", "test", "test"]})
    labels = pd.DataFrame([
        {"case_id": "a", "label": "RELEVANT", "adjudication_status": "model_agreed", "is_repeat": False},
        {"case_id": "a", "label": "RELEVANT", "adjudication_status": "blind_repeat", "is_repeat": True},
        {"case_id": "b", "label": "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE", "adjudication_status": "founder_adjudicated", "is_repeat": False},
        {"case_id": "c", "label": "NOT_RELEVANT", "adjudication_status": "founder_confirmed", "is_repeat": False},
        {"case_id": "d", "label": "NOT_RELEVANT", "adjudication_status": "founder_adjudicated", "is_repeat": False},
    ])
    assert d.label_counts_by_split(splits, labels) == {
        "train": {"labelled": 2, "resolved": 1, "unresolved": 1},
        "validation": {"labelled": 1, "resolved": 1, "unresolved": 0},
        "test": {"labelled": 1, "resolved": 1, "unresolved": 0},
    }


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def dagster_world(tmp_path_factory):
    from ucc_ml import cli
    from ucc_ml.synthetic import make_world, write_config, write_world

    root = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("dagster-world")
    write_world(make_world(), root)
    config_path = write_config(root)
    calls: list[list[str]] = []
    real_main = cli.main
    with pytest.MonkeyPatch.context() as patch:
        patch.setattr(cli, "main", lambda argv=None: calls.append(list(argv)) or real_main(argv))
        result = _materialize(MODEL_HALF, run_config=_confirm("final_evaluation_report", "release_bundle"),
                              config_path=str(config_path))
    return config_path, result, calls


def test_dagster_run_is_the_shell_run(dagster_world):
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config
    from ucc_ml.inference import load_release_bundle
    from ucc_ml.provenance import read_json

    config_path, result, calls = dagster_world
    assert result.success, _failures(result)
    paths = artefact_paths(load_config(config_path))
    releases = [p for p in paths.releases_dir.iterdir() if p.is_dir()]
    assert len(releases) == 1
    release, cfg = releases[0], str(config_path)
    assert calls == [
        ["train", "--config", cfg],
        ["freeze-candidate", "--config", cfg],
        ["evaluate-final", "--config", cfg],
        ["build-release", "--config", cfg],
        ["score-batch", "--config", cfg, "--release-dir", str(release)],
        ["export-public", "--config", cfg, "--release-dir", str(release)],
    ]
    assert load_release_bundle(release).release_id == release.name
    assert (paths.predictions_dir / f"{release.name}.parquet").is_file()
    assert read_json(paths.public_data_dir / "manifest.json")["current"] == release.name
    assert all(result.asset_materializations_for_node(name) for name in MODEL_HALF)


def test_the_single_test_guard_also_holds_inside_dagster(dagster_world):
    config_path, result, _ = dagster_world
    assert result.success, _failures(result)
    again = _materialize(["final_evaluation_report"], run_config=_confirm("final_evaluation_report"), config_path=str(config_path))
    assert again.success is False
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it to verify it fails**

Run:
```bash
./.venv-ml/bin/python -m pytest ml/tests/test_definitions.py -q 2>&1 | grep -E "ImportError|error"
```
Expected: `E   ImportError: cannot import name 'definitions' from 'ucc_ml' (…/ml/src/ucc_ml/__init__.py)` and `1 error in …` (collection error).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `ml/src/ucc_ml/definitions.py`:
```python
"""Dagster assets for the UCC ML Lab (Codex plan section 11; plan C Task 6).

    ./.venv-ml/bin/dagster dev -m ucc_ml.definitions            # from the repository root

Every asset that changes an artefact calls ``ucc_ml.cli.main(argv)``, the same entry point as
``./.venv-ml/bin/python -m ucc_ml.cli <command> --config ml/configs/v1.yaml``. A Dagster run is
therefore the same run as a shell run: same code, same guards (``evaluate-final`` still refuses a
second TEST run), same artefact paths from ``ucc_ml.config.artefact_paths``. Assets that stand for a
human or out-of-environment step (the snapshot export under ``.venv``, blind labelling and founder
review, TEST labels) only read what that step produced, through the owners' readers, and fail with
instructions when it is missing.

``final_evaluation_report`` and ``release_bundle`` are manual: they refuse to run unless the launch
config sets ``confirm_manual_run: true``. There is no schedule and no sensor, so nothing reads TEST
on its own. The config file comes from the ``ml_config`` resource: ``ml/configs/v1.yaml`` unless the
``UCC_ML_CONFIG`` environment variable names another.

No ``from __future__ import annotations`` here: Dagster resolves each asset's ``config`` parameter
from its real annotation object, and string annotations make it reject the Config classes.
"""
import os
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

import dagster as dg

DEFAULT_CONFIG = "ml/configs/v1.yaml"
GROUP = "ucc_ml"
LABEL_ROUNDS = ("pilot_v1", "main_v1")


class MlConfigResource(dg.ConfigurableResource):
    """Where the run config lives; every artefact path is derived from it."""

    config_path: str = DEFAULT_CONFIG


class ManualRunConfig(dg.Config):
    confirm_manual_run: bool = False


class LabelQueueConfig(dg.Config):
    labelling_round: str = "pilot_v1"
    chunk_size: int = 200


class ReleaseConfig(dg.Config):
    release_dir: str = ""


def _run_cli(argv: Sequence[str]) -> int:
    """Run one ucc_ml CLI command in-process and return its exit code (argparse errors exit 2)."""
    from ucc_ml import cli

    try:
        return int(cli.main(list(argv)))
    except SystemExit as exc:
        if exc.code is None:
            return 0
        return exc.code if isinstance(exc.code, int) else 1


def _command(argv: Sequence[str]) -> str:
    return "python -m ucc_ml.cli " + " ".join(argv)


def run_cli_step(argv: Sequence[str]) -> str:
    """Run the command; raise dg.Failure naming it and its exit code unless it exits 0."""
    code = _run_cli(argv)
    if code != 0:
        raise dg.Failure(
            description=f"`{_command(argv)}` exited {code}; the same command fails the same way from a shell.",
            metadata={"command": _command(argv), "exit_code": code},
        )
    return _command(argv)


def _result(commands: Sequence[str], **metadata) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    return dg.MaterializeResult(metadata={"commands": "\n".join(commands), **metadata})


def require_manual_confirmation(asset_name: str, config: ManualRunConfig) -> None:
    if not config.confirm_manual_run:
        raise dg.Failure(
            description=(f"{asset_name} is a manual run (Codex section 11): launch it with config "
                         "`confirm_manual_run: true`. Nothing was executed."))


def _artefacts(ml_config: MlConfigResource):
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config

    return artefact_paths(load_config(Path(ml_config.config_path)))


def label_counts_by_split(splits, labels) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
    """Labelled / resolved / unresolved rows per split. Only non-repeat rows with a resolved
    adjudication status count; resolved means RELEVANT or NOT_RELEVANT."""
    from ucc_ml.contracts import RESOLVED_ADJUDICATION_STATUSES

    kept = labels[(~labels["is_repeat"].astype(bool)) & labels["adjudication_status"].isin(list(RESOLVED_ADJUDICATION_STATUSES))]
    joined = kept.merge(splits[["case_id", "split"]], on="case_id", how="inner")
    counts: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    for split in ("train", "validation", "test"):
        part = joined[joined["split"] == split]
        resolved = int(part["label"].isin(["RELEVANT", "NOT_RELEVANT"]).sum())
        counts[split] = {"labelled": int(len(part)), "resolved": resolved, "unresolved": int(len(part)) - resolved}
    return counts


def _label_counts(ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
    from ucc_ml.labeling import read_labels
    from ucc_ml.splitting import read_splits

    paths = _artefacts(ml_config)
    return label_counts_by_split(read_splits(paths.splits_parquet), read_labels(paths.labels_csv))


def current_release_dir(config_path: Path) -> Path:
    """The release built from the current frozen candidate: the one bundle under releases_dir whose
    model-manifest.json and threshold.json equal the frozen candidate's byte for byte and whose
    directory name is its computed release id."""
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config
    from ucc_ml.inference import compute_release_id

    paths = artefact_paths(load_config(Path(config_path)))
    frozen = {name: (paths.frozen_dir / name).read_bytes() for name in ("model-manifest.json", "threshold.json")}
    matches: list[Path] = []
    if paths.releases_dir.is_dir():
        for candidate in sorted(p for p in paths.releases_dir.iterdir() if p.is_dir()):
            same = all((candidate / name).is_file() and (candidate / name).read_bytes() == data for name, data in frozen.items())
            if same and compute_release_id(candidate) == candidate.name:
                matches.append(candidate)
    if len(matches) != 1:
        raise dg.Failure(
            description=(f"expected exactly one release built from the frozen candidate under {paths.releases_dir}, "
                         f"found {[m.name for m in matches]}; set release_dir in the launch config"))
    return matches[0]


def _release_dir(config: ReleaseConfig, ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> Path:
    return Path(config.release_dir) if config.release_dir else current_release_dir(Path(ml_config.config_path))


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, description="Snapshot manifest written by ml/tools/export_snapshot.py under .venv (DuckDB 1.4.5). Checked here, never produced.")
def source_snapshot_manifest(ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    from ucc_ml.provenance import sha256_file

    path = _artefacts(ml_config).snapshot_manifest
    if not path.is_file():
        raise dg.Failure(
            description=(f"{path} is missing. Export the snapshot first, under the source venv: "
                         "./.venv/bin/python ml/tools/export_snapshot.py --source ucc.duckdb --out ml/data/snapshots/v1"))
    return dg.MaterializeResult(metadata={"path": str(path), "sha256": sha256_file(path)})


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[source_snapshot_manifest], description="build-candidates: candidates.parquet and the count reconciliation.")
def candidate_cases(ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    return _result([run_cli_step(["build-candidates", "--config", ml_config.config_path])])


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[candidate_cases], description="make-pilot: the stratified 200-case pilot draw and its inclusion probabilities.")
def grouping_and_sampling_manifest(ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    return _result([run_cli_step(["make-pilot", "--config", ml_config.config_path])])


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[grouping_and_sampling_manifest], description="freeze-splits: grouped splits with every pilot group in train.")
def split_manifest(ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    return _result([run_cli_step(["freeze-splits", "--config", ml_config.config_path])])


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[grouping_and_sampling_manifest, split_manifest],
          description="label-blind for one round (main_v1 is drawn with make-main-round first). The blind passes and the founder label outside Dagster.")
def labeling_queue(config: LabelQueueConfig, ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    if config.labelling_round not in LABEL_ROUNDS:
        raise dg.Failure(description=f"labelling_round must be one of {LABEL_ROUNDS}, got {config.labelling_round!r}")
    commands = []
    argv = ["label-blind", "--config", ml_config.config_path, "--round", config.labelling_round]
    if config.labelling_round == "main_v1":
        commands.append(run_cli_step(["make-main-round", "--config", ml_config.config_path]))
        argv += ["--chunk-size", str(config.chunk_size)]
    commands.append(run_cli_step(argv))
    return _result(commands, labelling_round=config.labelling_round)


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[labeling_queue],
          description="validate-labels: merges the rounds into labels.csv; refuses while any disagreement lacks a founder decision.")
def approved_labels(ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    return _result([run_cli_step(["validate-labels", "--config", ml_config.config_path])])


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[approved_labels, split_manifest],
          description="Reads candidates, splits and labels through their owners' readers and records TRAIN and VALIDATION label counts.")
def training_dataset(ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    from ucc_ml.dataset import read_candidates

    candidates = read_candidates(_artefacts(ml_config).candidates_parquet)
    counts = _label_counts(ml_config)
    if counts["train"]["resolved"] == 0:
        raise dg.Failure(description="TRAIN has no resolved (RELEVANT / NOT_RELEVANT) labels; finish labelling and validate-labels first.")
    metadata = {f"{split}_{kind}": value for split in ("train", "validation") for kind, value in counts[split].items()}
    return dg.MaterializeResult(metadata={"candidates": int(len(candidates)), **metadata})


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[training_dataset], description="train: grouped CV inside TRAIN, refit, out-of-fold scores, MLflow.")
def trained_candidate(ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    return _result([run_cli_step(["train", "--config", ml_config.config_path])])


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[trained_candidate], description="freeze-candidate: calibration on OOF scores, threshold and validation report on VALIDATION.")
def validation_report(ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    return _result([run_cli_step(["freeze-candidate", "--config", ml_config.config_path])])


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[validation_report], description="Verifies the frozen candidate's SHA256SUMS and records its threshold and status.")
def frozen_candidate_manifest(ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    from ucc_ml.provenance import read_json, verify_sha256sums

    frozen_dir = _artefacts(ml_config).frozen_dir
    try:
        digests = verify_sha256sums(frozen_dir, required=("model-manifest.json", "threshold.json"))
    except (FileNotFoundError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise dg.Failure(description=f"the frozen candidate in {frozen_dir} does not verify: {exc}") from exc
    threshold = read_json(frozen_dir / "threshold.json")
    return dg.MaterializeResult(metadata={"files": len(digests), "threshold": float(threshold["threshold"]), "status": str(threshold["status"])})


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[approved_labels, split_manifest],
          description="Checks TEST has resolved labels before the manual final evaluation. Records counts only; never scores TEST.")
def final_test_labels(ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    counts = _label_counts(ml_config)["test"]
    if counts["resolved"] == 0:
        raise dg.Failure(description="TEST has no resolved labels; the final evaluation cannot run.")
    return dg.MaterializeResult(metadata={f"test_{kind}": value for kind, value in counts.items()})


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[frozen_candidate_manifest, final_test_labels],
          description="MANUAL. evaluate-final: scores TEST once. Requires confirm_manual_run: true.")
def final_evaluation_report(config: ManualRunConfig, ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    require_manual_confirmation("final_evaluation_report", config)
    return _result([run_cli_step(["evaluate-final", "--config", ml_config.config_path])])


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[final_evaluation_report],
          description="MANUAL. build-release: the immutable release bundle. Requires confirm_manual_run: true.")
def release_bundle(config: ManualRunConfig, ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    require_manual_confirmation("release_bundle", config)
    command = run_cli_step(["build-release", "--config", ml_config.config_path])
    release_dir = current_release_dir(Path(ml_config.config_path))
    return _result([command], release_id=release_dir.name)


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[release_bundle], description="score-batch: every candidate scored by the release.")
def batch_predictions(config: ReleaseConfig, ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    release_dir = _release_dir(config, ml_config)
    command = run_cli_step(["score-batch", "--config", ml_config.config_path, "--release-dir", str(release_dir)])
    return _result([command], release_id=release_dir.name)


@dg.asset(group_name=GROUP, deps=[batch_predictions], description="export-public: the whitelisted documents under the public data directory.")
def public_exports(config: ReleaseConfig, ml_config: MlConfigResource) -> dg.MaterializeResult:
    release_dir = _release_dir(config, ml_config)
    command = run_cli_step(["export-public", "--config", ml_config.config_path, "--release-dir", str(release_dir)])
    return _result([command], release_id=release_dir.name)


ASSETS = [
    source_snapshot_manifest, candidate_cases, grouping_and_sampling_manifest, split_manifest, labeling_queue,
    approved_labels, training_dataset, trained_candidate, validation_report, frozen_candidate_manifest,
    final_test_labels, final_evaluation_report, release_bundle, batch_predictions, public_exports,
]

defs = dg.Definitions(
    assets=ASSETS,
    resources={"ml_config": MlConfigResource(config_path=os.environ.get("UCC_ML_CONFIG", DEFAULT_CONFIG))},
)
```

```bash
ml(c): Dagster assets that run the same CLI entry point as the shell

ucc_ml.definitions declares the 15 assets of the Codex plan section 11 with deps-only edges.
Every asset that changes an artefact calls ucc_ml.cli.main(argv) with the exact argv a person
would type (build-candidates, make-pilot, freeze-splits, make-main-round + label-blind,
validate-labels, train, freeze-candidate, evaluate-final, build-release, score-batch,
export-public), so a Dagster run is the same run as a shell run: same code, same artefact
paths from artefact_paths, same guards. A non-zero exit fails the asset with the command and
its exit code.

Human and out-of-environment steps are explicit dependencies, not fake automation:
source_snapshot_manifest only checks the snapshot exported under .venv; approved_labels is
validate-labels, which refuses while any disagreement lacks a founder decision;
training_dataset and final_test_labels read the artefacts through the K7 readers and record
counts, never scores.

final_evaluation_report and release_bundle refuse to run unless the launch config sets
confirm_manual_run: true, and nothing was executed when they refuse. There is no schedule and
no sensor. batch_predictions and public_exports serve the release whose model-manifest.json and
threshold.json match the frozen candidate, or an explicit release_dir.

without confirmation and run evaluate-final with it; a failing command names its exit code; and
one in-process materialisation of the model half on a fresh synthetic world issues exactly the
six shell commands, yields a loadable release, its predictions and a public manifest naming it.
A second confirmed final evaluation then fails inside Dagster, because the single-TEST-run guard
is the CLI's own.
```

```python
"""ml/DEPLOY.md is the operating record for the public side of the Lab (plan C Task 7). These checks
pin the commands that must not drift: the Pages source check, the scoped gh login, the release
export, the preview outside docs/, the explicit-path commit and a rollback with nothing to fill in."""
from pathlib import Path

DOC = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "ml" / "DEPLOY.md"


def test_deploy_doc_pins_the_static_publishing_commands():
    text = DOC.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for required in (
        "gh api repos/drkyoyodating/UCC-Demo/pages --jq '.status, .source.branch, .source.path, .html_url'",
        "It prints `built`, `main`, `/docs` and `https://drkyoyodating.github.io/UCC-Demo/`",
        "gh auth login --hostname github.com --git-protocol https --web --scopes workflow",
        "./.venv-ml/bin/python ml/tools/make_lab_preview.py --out /tmp/ucc-lab-preview",
        'RELEASE_ID=$(sed -n \'s/^release_id=//p\' docs/data/ml/release_v1.sha256)',
        './.venv-ml/bin/python -m ucc_ml.cli export-public --config ml/configs/v1.yaml --release-dir "ml/artifacts/releases/$RELEASE_ID"',
        "never `git add -A`",
        'git revert --no-edit "$(git log -1 --format=%H -- docs/data/ml/manifest.json)"',
        "https://drkyoyodating.github.io/UCC-Demo/ml/",
    ):
        assert required in text, required


def test_deploy_doc_names_nothing_private():
    text = DOC.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "UCC_DEMO_RUNBOOK" not in text
    # No trailing slash: the marker must survive Task 1 Step 8's sanitiser (and it matches more).
    assert "/Users" not in text
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the operating record**

Create `ml/DEPLOY.md`:
```markdown
# Deploying the UCC ML Lab

The operating record for the public side of the ML Lab: the static page on GitHub Pages, the CI
check, and the live prediction API on Cloud Run, which exists only if the founder approves it.
Every command runs from the repository root.

## 1. What is public

The GitHub Pages site is the `docs/` directory of `main`, served at
https://drkyoyodating.github.io/UCC-Demo/. The Lab page is `docs/ml/index.html` with
`docs/assets/ml-lab.js` and `docs/assets/ml-lab.css`, served at
https://drkyoyodating.github.io/UCC-Demo/ml/. Its data is `docs/data/ml/manifest.json` plus
`docs/data/ml/releases/<release_id>/metrics.json`, `examples.json` and `model-card.json`, and only
`export-public` writes those files: a whitelist of the release's metrics, the curated examples and
the model card.

Never public: `ml/data/`, `ml/artifacts/`, `ml/mlflow/` and `ml/reports/` (all gitignored), the
labels, per-case predictions and the release bundle itself. The map page links to the Lab once, in
its footer; the Lab never changes `scope_all`, the headline counts or the map.

## 2. Pages source

The Pages source was checked with the GitHub API before the first Lab release:

    gh api repos/drkyoyodating/UCC-Demo/pages --jq '.status, .source.branch, .source.path, .html_url'

It prints `built`, `main`, `/docs` and `https://drkyoyodating.github.io/UCC-Demo/`, one per line.
If that ever changes, stop: every path in this file assumes it.

## 3. GitHub CLI authentication

Pushing a file under `.github/workflows/` over HTTPS needs a token with the `workflow` scope. The
founder signs in once, in his own terminal:

    gh auth login --hostname github.com --git-protocol https --web --scopes workflow

and answers yes to "Authenticate Git with your GitHub credentials?", so `git push` uses the same
token. `gh auth status` then lists `workflow` among the token scopes.

## 4. Previewing the layout without real data

    ./.venv-ml/bin/python ml/tools/make_lab_preview.py --out /tmp/ucc-lab-preview
    ./.venv-ml/bin/python -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1 --directory /tmp/ucc-lab-preview/site

Open http://127.0.0.1:8080/ml/. The preview runs the real pipeline on the fictitious synthetic
world, shows a SYNTHETIC banner, and is never copied under `docs/`.

## 5. Publishing a release to the static page

Plan B has built `ml/artifacts/releases/<release_id>/`, scored every candidate with `score-batch`,
and committed `docs/data/ml/release_v1.sha256`, whose first line is `release_id=<release_id>`.
Export the public documents for exactly that release:

    RELEASE_ID=$(sed -n 's/^release_id=//p' docs/data/ml/release_v1.sha256)
    ./.venv-ml/bin/python -m ucc_ml.cli export-public --config ml/configs/v1.yaml --release-dir "ml/artifacts/releases/$RELEASE_ID"

Check the result locally before anything is committed:

    ./.venv-ml/bin/python -m pytest ml/tests/test_lab_page.py -q
    ./.venv-ml/bin/python -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1 --directory docs

Open http://127.0.0.1:8080/ml/ and http://127.0.0.1:8080/. The founder approves the layout with
the real numbers and the curated example list (named businesses appear next to their labels)
before the files are committed. Commit by explicit path, never `git add -A`, then push and confirm
the live page:

    git add docs/data/ml/manifest.json "docs/data/ml/releases/$RELEASE_ID/metrics.json" "docs/data/ml/releases/$RELEASE_ID/examples.json" "docs/data/ml/releases/$RELEASE_ID/model-card.json"
    git push origin main
    gh run watch "$(gh run list --workflow pages-build-deployment --branch main --limit 1 --json databaseId --jq '.[0].databaseId')" --exit-status
    curl -sS -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' https://drkyoyodating.github.io/UCC-Demo/ml/

## 6. Continuous integration

`.github/workflows/ml-checks.yml` installs `ml/requirements-ml.lock.txt` on Python 3.14, runs
`pytest ml/tests` on synthetic fixtures only, and checks `docs/assets/ml-lab.js` with node 22. It
never reads `ml/data/` or the database, and a green run says nothing about model accuracy.

    gh run watch "$(gh run list --workflow ml-checks.yml --branch main --limit 1 --json databaseId --jq '.[0].databaseId')" --exit-status

## 7. Rolling back the static page

A release's public files are ordinary commits, so the previous state of the page is one revert
away. This reverts the most recent change to the manifest, which moves `current` back with it:

    git revert --no-edit "$(git log -1 --format=%H -- docs/data/ml/manifest.json)"
    git push origin main

## 8. Live API

Not deployed. Plan C Task 11 records the founder's decision in `ml/deploy_decision.json`. If he
approves a deployment, plan C Task 12 replaces this section with the Cloud Run procedure, its
rollback and its disable switch.
```

### Task 8: GitHub Actions `ml-checks` — synthetic tests on the locked environment, pushed and watched

**Files:**
- Create: `.github/workflows/ml-checks.yml`
- Test: `ml/tests/test_ci_workflow.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: A Task 1's `ml/requirements-ml.lock.txt` (pip freeze of `.venv-ml`: 131 lines, no URL or editable lines, so it installs as-is), `ml/pyproject.toml` and `ml/README.md`; every suite under `ml/tests` (synthetic fixtures only); Task 5's `docs/assets/ml-lab.js` and `ml/tests/js/test_ml_lab.cjs`; Task 7's `gh` session with the `workflow` scope.
- Produces: the workflow `ml-checks` with job `checks` on `ubuntu-latest`. It runs on a push to `main` or a pull request that touches `ml/**`, `docs/ml/**`, `docs/assets/ml-lab.*`, `docs/data/ml/**`, `docs/index.html` or the workflow itself, and on manual dispatch. Tasks 10 and 12 watch it after their pushes.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `ml/tests/test_ci_workflow.py`:
```python
"""The CI workflow (plan C Task 8): triggered by ML paths only, read-only token, the locked
environment on Python 3.14, synthetic tests and the Lab page script, and nothing private."""
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

WORKFLOW = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / ".github" / "workflows" / "ml-checks.yml"
ML_PATHS = {"ml/**", "docs/ml/**", "docs/assets/ml-lab.*", "docs/data/ml/**", "docs/index.html", ".github/workflows/ml-checks.yml"}


def _load() -> tuple[dict, dict]:
    doc = yaml.safe_load(WORKFLOW.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    # PyYAML follows YAML 1.1, where the bare key `on` is the boolean True.
    return doc, doc.get("on", doc.get(True))


def test_triggers_and_permissions():
    doc, triggers = _load()
    assert set(triggers) == {"push", "pull_request", "workflow_dispatch"}
    assert triggers["push"]["branches"] == ["main"]
    for event in ("push", "pull_request"):
        assert set(triggers[event]["paths"]) == ML_PATHS, event
    assert doc["permissions"] == {"contents": "read"}


def test_steps_install_the_lock_then_run_synthetic_checks():
    doc, _ = _load()
    job = doc["jobs"]["checks"]
    assert job["runs-on"] == "ubuntu-latest"
    steps = job["steps"]
    assert all("@v" in step["uses"] for step in steps if "uses" in step)
    python = next(step for step in steps if step.get("uses", "").startswith("actions/setup-python@"))
    assert python["with"]["python-version"] == "3.14"
    node_index = next(i for i, step in enumerate(steps) if step.get("uses", "").startswith("actions/setup-node@"))
    assert str(steps[node_index]["with"]["node-version"]) == "22"
    runs = [step.get("run", "") for step in steps]
    pytest_index = next(i for i, run in enumerate(runs) if "python -m pytest ml/tests" in run)
    assert node_index < pytest_index, "node must be installed before pytest (test_lab_page runs the node tests)"
    script = "\n".join(runs)
    for command in (
        "python -m pip install -r ml/requirements-ml.lock.txt",
        "python -m pip install --no-deps -e ml",
        "python -m pytest ml/tests -q -p no:cacheprovider",
        "node --check docs/assets/ml-lab.js",
        "node --test ml/tests/js/test_ml_lab.cjs",
    ):
        assert command in script, command


def test_workflow_touches_nothing_private():
    text = WORKFLOW.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for private in ("secrets.", "ucc.duckdb", "ml/data", "ml/artifacts", "gcloud"):
        assert private not in text, private
```



Create `.github/workflows/ml-checks.yml`:
```yaml
# ML Lab checks (plan C Task 8). Synthetic fixtures only: this workflow never downloads the snapshot
# or any private data, and a green run is not evidence of model accuracy.
name: ml-checks

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - "ml/**"
      - "docs/ml/**"
      - "docs/assets/ml-lab.*"
      - "docs/data/ml/**"
      - "docs/index.html"
      - ".github/workflows/ml-checks.yml"
  pull_request:
    paths:
      - "ml/**"
      - "docs/ml/**"
      - "docs/assets/ml-lab.*"
      - "docs/data/ml/**"
      - "docs/index.html"
      - ".github/workflows/ml-checks.yml"
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: ml-checks-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  checks:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 30
    env:
      MLFLOW_DISABLE_AGENT_HINT: "1"
      PIP_DISABLE_PIP_VERSION_CHECK: "1"
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v5

      - uses: actions/setup-python@v6
        with:
          python-version: "3.14"
          cache: pip
          cache-dependency-path: ml/requirements-ml.lock.txt

      - uses: actions/setup-node@v5
        with:
          node-version: "22"

      - name: Install the locked ML environment
        run: |
          python -m pip install -r ml/requirements-ml.lock.txt
          python -m pip install --no-deps -e ml
          python -m pip check

      - name: ML tests (synthetic fixtures only)
        run: python -m pytest ml/tests -q -p no:cacheprovider

      - name: Lab page script
        run: |
          node --check docs/assets/ml-lab.js
          node --test ml/tests/js/test_ml_lab.cjs
```

```bash
A GitHub Actions workflow for the ML Lab, triggered only by ML paths (ml/**, docs/ml/**,
```

### Task 9: The Cloud Run image definition, rehearsed locally without Docker

**Files:**
- Create: `ml/Dockerfile`
- Create: `ml/.dockerignore`
- Create: `ml/.gcloudignore`
- Create: `ml/requirements-serve.txt`
- Test: `ml/tests/test_serving_image.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - C Task 4's `ucc_ml.api.app_from_env() -> FastAPI`, which reads `UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR`, with `GET /readyz` → `{"status": "ready", "release_id": …}` once loaded, `GET /v1/model` → `{"release_id": …, …}`, and `POST /v1/predict` → exactly `request_id, release_id, input_hash, score, score_type, decision, threshold, baseline_qualifies, baseline_route, top_feature_contributions, elapsed_ms`, where each contribution is `{block, feature, contribution}` (K8).
  - K8 `load_release_bundle`, which requires a bundle's directory name to equal its release id; the K10 fixture `synthetic_release_dir`.
  - A Task 1's `ml/pyproject.toml`, `ml/README.md` and `ml/requirements-ml.lock.txt`; `packaging` (in the lock).
- Produces:
  - The build definition Task 12 deploys with `gcloud run deploy ucc-ml-api --source ml` (Cloud Build, no local Docker).
  - The container command `sh -c "exec uvicorn --factory ucc_ml.api:app_from_env --host 0.0.0.0 --port ${PORT:-8080}"`.
  - Bundles at `/app/releases/<release_id>/`, so the name check holds; the served release is picked per revision with `--set-env-vars UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR=/app/releases/<release_id>`.
  - `ml/requirements-serve.txt`: distribution names only, installed with `-c requirements-ml.lock.txt`, so the image carries exactly the trained versions and no MLflow or Dagster.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `ml/tests/test_serving_image.py`:
```python
"""The Cloud Run image (plan C Task 9), rehearsed without Docker: the Dockerfile's shape, what the
build context may contain, that requirements-serve.txt names only locked packages, and a live run
of the container command under uvicorn against the synthetic release while every import outside
the serve requirements' dependency closure is blocked."""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.metadata as metadata
import json
import os
import re
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from packaging.requirements import Requirement
from packaging.utils import canonicalize_name

ML = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DOCKERFILE = ML / "Dockerfile"
DOCKERIGNORE = ML / ".dockerignore"
GCLOUDIGNORE = ML / ".gcloudignore"
SERVE = ML / "requirements-serve.txt"
LOCK = ML / "requirements-ml.lock.txt"
CONTAINER_COMMAND = "exec uvicorn --factory ucc_ml.api:app_from_env --host 0.0.0.0 --port ${PORT:-8080}"
PRIVATE_ENTRIES = ("data/", "mlflow/", "reports/", "tests/", "artifacts/frozen/", "artifacts/final_eval/", "artifacts/train/")
NEEDED_ENTRIES = ("Dockerfile", ".dockerignore", "pyproject.toml", "README.md", "requirements-serve.txt",
                  "requirements-ml.lock.txt", "src", "src/", "artifacts/", "artifacts/releases/")
PACK_FIELDS = {"request_id", "release_id", "input_hash", "score", "score_type", "decision", "threshold",
               "baseline_qualifies", "baseline_route", "top_feature_contributions", "elapsed_ms"}

CHILD = r'''
import importlib.abc, importlib.metadata, json, re, runpy, sys

allowed = set(json.loads(sys.argv[1]))
owners = importlib.metadata.packages_distributions()


def canonical(name):
    return re.sub(r"[-_.]+", "-", name).lower()


class ServeOnly(importlib.abc.MetaPathFinder):
    def find_spec(self, fullname, path=None, target=None):
        top = fullname.partition(".")[0]
        if top == "ucc_ml" or top in sys.stdlib_module_names:
            return None
        distributions = owners.get(top)
        if distributions and not any(canonical(d) in allowed for d in distributions):
            raise ModuleNotFoundError(f"{fullname} comes from {distributions}, which requirements-serve.txt does not install")
        return None


sys.meta_path.insert(0, ServeOnly())
sys.argv = ["uvicorn", "--factory", "ucc_ml.api:app_from_env", "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", sys.argv[2]]
runpy.run_module("uvicorn", run_name="__main__", alter_sys=True)
'''


def serve_requirements() -> list[Requirement]:
    lines = [line.strip() for line in SERVE.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    return [Requirement(line) for line in lines if line and not line.startswith("#")]


def locked_versions() -> dict[str, str]:
    pins: dict[str, str] = {}
    for line in LOCK.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        name, separator, version = line.strip().partition("==")
        if separator:
            pins[canonicalize_name(name)] = version
    return pins


def serve_closure() -> set[str]:
    """Every distribution the serve requirements install, following Requires-Dist (and extras) here."""
    seen: set[tuple[str, frozenset[str]]] = set()
    names: set[str] = set()
    queue = [(requirement.name, frozenset(requirement.extras)) for requirement in serve_requirements()]
    while queue:
        name, extras = queue.pop()
        key = (canonicalize_name(name), extras)
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        names.add(key[0])
        environments = [{"extra": extra} for extra in sorted(extras)] or [{"extra": ""}]
        for spec in metadata.distribution(name).requires or []:
            requirement = Requirement(spec)
            if requirement.marker is None or any(requirement.marker.evaluate(env) for env in environments):
                queue.append((requirement.name, frozenset(requirement.extras)))
    return names


def ignore_entries(path: Path) -> set[str]:
    return {line.strip() for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if line.strip() and not line.startswith("#")}


def test_dockerfile_installs_locked_serve_requirements_the_package_and_the_bundles():
    text = DOCKERFILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert re.findall(r"^FROM (.+)$", text, re.M) == ["python:3.14-slim"]
    assert "RUN python -m pip install -r requirements-serve.txt -c requirements-ml.lock.txt" in text
    assert "RUN python -m pip install --no-deps ." in text
    assert re.findall(r"^COPY (.+)$", text, re.M) == [
        "requirements-serve.txt requirements-ml.lock.txt ./",
        "pyproject.toml README.md ./",
        "src/ ./src/",
        "artifacts/releases/ /app/releases/",
    ]
    assert "USER ucc" in text
    assert "ENV UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR" not in text, "the served release is chosen per revision at deploy time"
    assert json.loads(re.search(r"^CMD (.+)$", text, re.M).group(1)) == ["sh", "-c", CONTAINER_COMMAND]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("ignore_file", [DOCKERIGNORE, GCLOUDIGNORE], ids=lambda p: p.name)
def test_build_context_leaves_private_artefacts_behind(ignore_file):
    entries = ignore_entries(ignore_file)
    for private in PRIVATE_ENTRIES:
        assert private in entries, private
    for needed in NEEDED_ENTRIES:
        assert needed not in entries, needed


def test_serve_requirements_are_unpinned_names_and_all_locked():
    pins = locked_versions()
    requirements = serve_requirements()
    names = {canonicalize_name(requirement.name) for requirement in requirements}
    assert {"fastapi", "uvicorn", "pydantic", "scikit-learn", "pandas", "numpy", "joblib"} <= names
    for requirement in requirements:
        assert not requirement.specifier, f"{requirement}: versions come from the lock file (-c), not from here"
        assert canonicalize_name(requirement.name) in pins, requirement.name
    closure = serve_closure()
    assert closure <= set(pins), sorted(closure - set(pins))
    assert "mlflow" not in closure and "dagster" not in closure


def _free_port() -> int:
    with socket.socket() as sock:
        sock.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        return sock.getsockname()[1]


def _call(url: str, body: dict | None = None) -> tuple[int, dict]:
    data = json.dumps(body).encode("utf-8") if body is not None else None
    request = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, headers={"content-type": "application/json"} if data else {})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=10) as response:
            return response.status, json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
        return error.code, json.loads(error.read().decode("utf-8") or "{}")


def test_container_command_serves_the_synthetic_release_with_serve_requirements_only(synthetic_release_dir):
    port = _free_port()
    env = dict(os.environ, UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR=str(synthetic_release_dir), PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1")
    child = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-c", CHILD, json.dumps(sorted(serve_closure())), str(port)],
                             env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)
    base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
    try:
        status, body = 0, {}
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 90
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            if child.poll() is not None:
                pytest.fail("the container command exited early:\n" + child.stdout.read()[-4000:])
            try:
                status, body = _call(base + "/readyz")
            except (urllib.error.URLError, ConnectionError, TimeoutError):
                status = 0
            if status == 200:
                break
            time.sleep(0.5)
        assert status == 200 and body.get("status") == "ready", body
        assert body["release_id"] == synthetic_release_dir.name
        status, model = _call(base + "/v1/model")
        assert status == 200 and model["release_id"] == synthetic_release_dir.name
        status, prediction = _call(base + "/v1/predict", {"borrower_name": "Example Excavation LLC",
                                                          "lender_names": ["Example Finance Company"], "region": "CO"})
        assert status == 200, prediction
        assert set(prediction) == PACK_FIELDS
        assert prediction["release_id"] == synthetic_release_dir.name
        assert all(set(item) == {"block", "feature", "contribution"} for item in prediction["top_feature_contributions"])
    finally:
        child.terminate()
        try:
            child.wait(timeout=20)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            child.kill()
```

```bash
./.venv-ml/bin/python -m pytest ml/tests/test_serving_image.py -q 2>&1 | tail -1
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the Dockerfile**

Create `ml/Dockerfile`:
```dockerfile
# UCC ML API image (plan C Task 9). Built by `gcloud run deploy ucc-ml-api --source ml` with Cloud
# Build; there is no local Docker. The image holds the ucc_ml package and the release bundles only:
# no candidates, labels, MLflow runs, frozen candidates or TEST predictions (see .dockerignore).
FROM python:3.14-slim

ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 \
    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 \
    PIP_NO_CACHE_DIR=1 \
    PIP_DISABLE_PIP_VERSION_CHECK=1

WORKDIR /app

# requirements-serve.txt lists names only; every version, and every transitive dependency, comes
# from the lock file the model was trained with.
COPY requirements-serve.txt requirements-ml.lock.txt ./
RUN python -m pip install -r requirements-serve.txt -c requirements-ml.lock.txt

COPY pyproject.toml README.md ./
COPY src/ ./src/
RUN python -m pip install --no-deps .

# Each bundle directory is named after its release_id and the loader checks that, so bundles keep
# their names. UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR=/app/releases/<release_id> is set per revision at deploy time.
COPY artifacts/releases/ /app/releases/

RUN useradd --system --uid 10001 --home-dir /app ucc && chown -R ucc /app
USER ucc

CMD ["sh", "-c", "exec uvicorn --factory ucc_ml.api:app_from_env --host 0.0.0.0 --port ${PORT:-8080}"]
```

Create `ml/.dockerignore`:

```text
# Build context is ml/ (gcloud run deploy --source ml). The image needs only Dockerfile,
# pyproject.toml, README.md, requirements-serve.txt, requirements-ml.lock.txt, src/ and
# artifacts/releases/. Everything private stays out of the context.
data/
mlflow/
reports/
tests/
tools/
specs/
configs/
artifacts/frozen/
artifacts/final_eval/
artifacts/train/
artifacts/releases/release-*
DEPLOY.md
deploy_decision.json
pytest.ini
**/__pycache__
**/*.pyc
**/*.egg-info
.pytest_cache/
```

```text
# labels, MLflow runs, frozen candidate and TEST predictions never leave this machine. Task 12 lists
# the upload with `gcloud meta list-files-for-upload ml` and stops if anything else appears.
.gcloudignore
.git
data/
mlflow/
reports/
tests/
tools/
specs/
configs/
artifacts/frozen/
artifacts/final_eval/
artifacts/train/
artifacts/releases/release-*/
DEPLOY.md

pytest.ini
__pycache__/
*.pyc
*.egg-info/
.pytest_cache/
```

```text
# exact version the model was trained with. ml/tests/test_serving_image.py serves the synthetic
# release with nothing importable beyond these packages and their dependencies.

uvicorn[standard]
pydantic
scikit-learn

numpy
pandas
pyarrow

PyYAML
```

```python
def test_map_page_links_to_the_lab_once_in_its_footer_and_outside_the_tabs():
    html = MAP_PAGE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert html.count('href="./ml/"') == 1
    link = html.index('<a href="./ml/">')
    foot_start = html.index('<p id="foot">')
    assert foot_start < link < html.index("</p>", foot_start), "the link sits in the footer paragraph"
    tabs_start = html.index('<div id="tabs">')
    assert not tabs_start < link < html.index("</div>", tabs_start), "the link is not a tab"
    assert 'data-p="ml"' not in html
    assert html.count('class="chip') == 6, "no new chip"
```

### Task 11: FOUNDER GATE — Google Cloud project, billing and budget, or skip the live API

**Files:**
- Create: `ml/tools/record_deploy_decision.py`
- Test: `ml/tests/test_deploy_decision.py`
- Create (written by the tool, then committed): `ml/deploy_decision.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: K6 `ucc_ml.provenance.read_json`, `write_json`, `utc_now_iso`; the live static Lab from Task 10.
- Produces: `ml/tools/record_deploy_decision.py` with `REPO_ROOT`, `DECISION_FILE = ml/deploy_decision.json`, `CLOUD_RUN: dict`, `build_record(decision: str, budget_usd: float | None, decided_at: str) -> dict`, `require(path: Path, expected: str) -> int` (0 on a match, 3 otherwise) and `main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int`. It also writes `ml/deploy_decision.json`: `{decision: "deploy", decided_at, budget_alert_usd_per_month, cloud_run, note}` or `{decision: "skip", decided_at, note}`. Task 12 begins with `record_deploy_decision.py --require deploy` and stops unless it exits 0.

Create `ml/tests/test_deploy_decision.py`:
```python
"""The live-API decision record (plan C Task 11): deploy needs a budget, skip takes none, nothing
private is written to the public repository, and --require is Task 12's precondition."""
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def tool():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("record_deploy_decision", REPO / "ml" / "tools" / "record_deploy_decision.py")
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def test_build_record_for_both_decisions(tool):
    deploy = tool.build_record("deploy", 10, "2026-09-12T10:00:00Z")
    assert deploy["decision"] == "deploy" and deploy["budget_alert_usd_per_month"] == 10.0
    assert deploy["cloud_run"] == {"service": "ucc-ml-api", "region": "us-central1", "memory": "1Gi", "cpu": 1,
                                   "min_instances": 0, "max_instances": 2, "concurrency": 8}
    skip = tool.build_record("skip", None, "2026-09-12T10:00:00Z")
    assert skip["decision"] == "skip" and "api_url null" in skip["note"]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="--budget-usd greater than 0"):
        tool.build_record("deploy", None, "t")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="--budget-usd greater than 0"):
        tool.build_record("deploy", 0, "t")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="takes no --budget-usd"):
        tool.build_record("skip", 5, "t")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="deploy or skip"):
        tool.build_record("later", None, "t")


def test_the_written_record_holds_nothing_private(tool, tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "deploy_decision.json"
    assert tool.main(["deploy", "--budget-usd", "25", "--path", str(path)]) == 0
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert set(json.loads(text)) == {"decision", "decided_at", "budget_alert_usd_per_month", "cloud_run", "note"}
    for private in ("project_id", "billing_account", "email", "@"):
        assert private not in text, private


def test_require_is_the_gate_task_12_checks(tool, tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "deploy_decision.json"
    assert tool.main(["--require", "deploy", "--path", str(path)]) == 3
    assert tool.main(["skip", "--path", str(path)]) == 0
    assert tool.main(["--require", "deploy", "--path", str(path)]) == 3
    assert tool.main(["--require", "skip", "--path", str(path)]) == 0
    assert tool.main(["deploy", "--path", str(path)]) == 2
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        tool.main(["--require", "deploy", "skip", "--path", str(path)])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        tool.main(["--path", str(path)])
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the tool**

Create `ml/tools/record_deploy_decision.py`:
```python
"""Record the founder's live-API decision (plan C Task 11) in ml/deploy_decision.json.

    ./.venv-ml/bin/python ml/tools/record_deploy_decision.py skip
    ./.venv-ml/bin/python ml/tools/record_deploy_decision.py deploy --budget-usd 10
    ./.venv-ml/bin/python ml/tools/record_deploy_decision.py --require deploy     # Task 12's precondition

The repository is public, so the record never holds the Google Cloud project id, the billing
account or the alert recipients; those stay in the Cloud Console and the local gcloud config.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
DECISION_FILE = REPO_ROOT / "ml" / "deploy_decision.json"
CLOUD_RUN = {"service": "ucc-ml-api", "region": "us-central1", "memory": "1Gi", "cpu": 1,
             "min_instances": 0, "max_instances": 2, "concurrency": 8}


def build_record(decision: str, budget_usd: float | None, decided_at: str) -> dict:
    if decision == "deploy":
        if budget_usd is None or not budget_usd > 0:
            raise ValueError("a deploy decision needs --budget-usd greater than 0 (the monthly budget alert amount)")
        return {
            "decision": "deploy",
            "decided_at": decided_at,
            "budget_alert_usd_per_month": float(budget_usd),
            "cloud_run": dict(CLOUD_RUN),
            "note": ("Budget alerts and max instances limit risk but are not hard spending caps. The project id, "
                     "billing account and alert recipients are deliberately not recorded in this public repository."),
        }
    if decision == "skip":
        if budget_usd is not None:
            raise ValueError("a skip decision takes no --budget-usd")
        return {
            "decision": "skip",
            "decided_at": decided_at,
            "note": ("The live API is not deployed. docs/data/ml/manifest.json keeps api_url null and the Lab page "
                     "says the API is unavailable."),
        }
    raise ValueError(f"decision must be deploy or skip, got {decision!r}")


def require(path: Path, expected: str) -> int:
    if not path.is_file():
        print(f"{path} does not exist: run the plan C Task 11 founder gate first", file=sys.stderr)
        return 3
    from ucc_ml.provenance import read_json

    actual = read_json(path).get("decision")
    if actual != expected:
        print(f"the recorded decision is {actual!r}, not {expected!r}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 3
    print(f"recorded decision: {actual}")
    return 0


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="record_deploy_decision", description="Record or check the live-API decision.")
    parser.add_argument("decision", nargs="?", choices=("deploy", "skip"))
    parser.add_argument("--budget-usd", type=float, default=None, help="monthly budget alert amount (deploy only)")
    parser.add_argument("--require", choices=("deploy", "skip"), default=None,
                        help="check the recorded decision instead of writing one; exit 3 when it differs")
    parser.add_argument("--path", type=Path, default=DECISION_FILE)
    ns = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if ns.require is not None:
        if ns.decision is not None or ns.budget_usd is not None:
            parser.error("--require takes no decision and no --budget-usd")
        return require(ns.path, ns.require)
    if ns.decision is None:
        parser.error("give a decision (deploy or skip) or --require")
    from ucc_ml.provenance import utc_now_iso, write_json

    try:
        record = build_record(ns.decision, ns.budget_usd, utc_now_iso())
    except ValueError as exc:
        print(str(exc), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    write_json(ns.path, record)
    print(f"recorded {record['decision']} in {ns.path}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

- [ ] **Step 5: FOUNDER GATE — present the facts, then ask one question**

Tell the founder, in these words:
- The static Lab is live and complete without an API. The form says the API is unavailable, and nothing else depends on it.
- The live API would be the Cloud Run service `ucc-ml-api` in `us-central1`: 1 vCPU, 1 GiB, 0 to 2 instances, concurrency 8, publicly callable with request bounds (one case, 300-character names, 20 lenders, 16 KB) and a per-client token bucket. No label-writing or training endpoint exists, and visitor inputs are not saved as feedback.
- Zero minimum instances means no idle instance cost and visible cold starts. Each deploy runs Cloud Build and stores an image in Artifact Registry, which costs a little storage.
- Budget alerts and maximum instances limit risk but are not hard spending caps. The disable switch is in `ml/DEPLOY.md`: clear `api_url`, remove public access, or delete the service.
- The repository is public, so the project id, billing account and alert e-mail will never be committed.

Ask exactly: "Deploy the live API? If yes: which Google Cloud project (existing or new), is billing linked to it, and what monthly budget alert amount in USD should I record? If no, the Lab stays static."

### Task 12: Deploy the live API to Cloud Run, enable the form, verify release parity, rehearse rollback

**Files:**
- Create: `ml/tools/set_manifest_api_url.py`
- Test: `ml/tests/test_set_manifest_api_url.py`
- Modify: `ml/DEPLOY.md` (section 8 becomes the live procedure)
- Modify: `ml/tests/test_deploy_doc.py` (append the live-procedure test)
- Modify (by the tool, then committed): `docs/data/ml/manifest.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 11's `ml/deploy_decision.json` (via `record_deploy_decision.py --require deploy`) and the founder's project with billing and a budget; Task 9's `ml/Dockerfile`, `ml/.dockerignore`, `ml/.gcloudignore`, `ml/requirements-serve.txt`; Task 10's `docs/data/ml/manifest.json` (`current` = release id, `api_url` null) and its `metrics.json`; C Task 4's endpoints `/healthz`, `/readyz`, `/v1/model`, `/v1/predict` with the pack's CORS origins and bounds; K6 `ucc_ml.provenance.read_json`, `write_json`, `utc_now_iso`; Task 5's page, which enables the form when `api_url` is an https origin whose `/readyz` answers 200.
- Produces: the Cloud Run service `ucc-ml-api` (us-central1) serving `/app/releases/<release_id>`; `docs/data/ml/manifest.json` with that release's `api_url` set to the service URL; `ml/tools/set_manifest_api_url.py` with `REPO_ROOT`, `MANIFEST`, `clean_api_url(value: str) -> str`, `set_api_url(manifest: dict, release_id: str, api_url: str | None, now: str) -> dict`, `main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int`; `ml/DEPLOY.md` section 8 with the deploy, verify, rollback, disable and cost-control procedure.

The deploy command is the pack's command plus two flags. `--set-env-vars UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR=/app/releases/<release_id>` picks the bundle the revision serves; the image holds every local bundle under its release-id name, because the loader checks that name. `--quiet` accepts, non-interactively, the creation of Cloud Run's `cloud-run-source-deploy` Artifact Registry repository on the first source deploy.

```python
"""Writing the live API URL into the public manifest (plan C Task 12): one field of one release
changes, only an https origin is accepted, and --clear is the disable switch."""

from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the tool**

Create `ml/tools/set_manifest_api_url.py`:
```python
"""Write or clear the live API URL of one release in docs/data/ml/manifest.json (plan C Task 12).

    ./.venv-ml/bin/python ml/tools/set_manifest_api_url.py --release-id "$RELEASE_ID" --api-url "$API_URL"
    ./.venv-ml/bin/python ml/tools/set_manifest_api_url.py --release-id "$RELEASE_ID" --clear

The Lab page enables its live form only for an https origin whose /readyz answers 200, and --clear
puts api_url back to null, which is the page's disable switch. Only that field (and generated_at,
when the manifest has one) changes, so the commit diff stays two lines.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import copy
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
MANIFEST = REPO_ROOT / "docs" / "data" / "ml" / "manifest.json"


def clean_api_url(value: str) -> str:
    url = value.strip().rstrip("/")
    parts = urlsplit(url)
    if parts.scheme != "https" or not parts.netloc or "@" in parts.netloc or parts.path or parts.query or parts.fragment:
        raise ValueError(f"api_url must be an https origin with no path, got {value!r}")
    return url


def set_api_url(manifest: dict, release_id: str, api_url: str | None, now: str) -> dict:
    updated = copy.deepcopy(manifest)
    entry = next((r for r in updated.get("releases", []) if r.get("release_id") == release_id), None)
    if entry is None:
        raise KeyError(f"release {release_id!r} is not in the manifest")
    entry["api_url"] = None if api_url is None else clean_api_url(api_url)
    if "generated_at" in updated:
        updated["generated_at"] = now
    return updated


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="set_manifest_api_url", description="Set or clear one release's api_url.")
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, default=MANIFEST)
    parser.add_argument("--release-id", required=True)
    target = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    target.add_argument("--api-url", help="the Cloud Run service URL, e.g. https://ucc-ml-api-abc123-uc.a.run.app")
    target.add_argument("--clear", action="store_true", help="set api_url to null (disables the live form)")
    ns = parser.parse_args(argv)
    from ucc_ml.provenance import read_json, utc_now_iso, write_json

    try:
        updated = set_api_url(read_json(ns.manifest), ns.release_id, None if ns.clear else ns.api_url, utc_now_iso())
    except (KeyError, ValueError) as exc:
        print(exc.args[0] if exc.args else exc, file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    write_json(ns.manifest, updated)
    entry = next(r for r in updated["releases"] if r["release_id"] == ns.release_id)
    print(f"{ns.release_id}: api_url = {entry['api_url']}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Replace the "Not deployed" section with the live procedure**

Replace in `ml/DEPLOY.md`:
```markdown
## 8. Live API

Not deployed. Plan C Task 11 records the founder's decision in `ml/deploy_decision.json`. If he
approves a deployment, plan C Task 12 replaces this section with the Cloud Run procedure, its
rollback and its disable switch.
```
```markdown
## 8. Live API

The prediction API runs on Cloud Run as the public service `ucc-ml-api` in `us-central1`: 1 vCPU,
1 GiB, 0 to 2 instances, concurrency 8. `ml/deploy_decision.json` records the founder's approval.
The Google Cloud project id, billing account and alert recipients live only in the Cloud Console
and the local gcloud config, never in this repository.

### 8.1 Deploy the current release

    RELEASE_ID=$(./.venv-ml/bin/python -c "import json; print(json.load(open('docs/data/ml/manifest.json'))['current'])")
    gcloud meta list-files-for-upload ml | sort
    gcloud run deploy ucc-ml-api --source ml --region us-central1 --memory 1Gi --cpu 1 --min-instances 0 --max-instances 2 --concurrency 8 --allow-unauthenticated --set-env-vars "UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR=/app/releases/$RELEASE_ID" --quiet
    API_URL=$(gcloud run services describe ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --format='value(status.url)')

Every uploaded path must be `Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore`, `pyproject.toml`, `README.md`,
`requirements-serve.txt`, `requirements-ml.lock.txt`, a file under `src/`, or a file under
`artifacts/releases/<release_id>/`. Anything else means stop. The image holds every local release
bundle; `UCC_ML_BUNDLE_DIR` picks the one a revision serves.

### 8.2 Verify, then enable the live form

    curl -sS "$API_URL/readyz"
    curl -sS "$API_URL/v1/model"
    ./.venv-ml/bin/python ml/tools/set_manifest_api_url.py --release-id "$RELEASE_ID" --api-url "$API_URL"
    git add docs/data/ml/manifest.json
    git commit -F - <<MSG
    ml(c): enable the live Lab form for release $RELEASE_ID

    docs/data/ml/manifest.json now carries the Cloud Run api_url for this release, written by
    ml/tools/set_manifest_api_url.py after /readyz and /v1/model both reported the manifest's
    current release id. The static report is unchanged and stays complete if the API goes away.

    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
    Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01T2qeexjws3qbeDg1WLsLab
    MSG
    git push origin main

`/readyz` and `/v1/model` must both report the manifest's `current` release id before the URL is
written. The page names both releases whenever they differ.

### 8.3 Roll back to the previous revision

    PREVIOUS=$(gcloud run revisions list --service ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --sort-by='~metadata.creationTimestamp' --format='value(metadata.name)' --limit 2 | tail -n 1)
    gcloud run services update-traffic ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --to-revisions "$PREVIOUS=100"
    gcloud run services describe ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --format='value(status.traffic)'

If the previous revision serves a different release from the static page, also roll the page back
(section 7) so the manifest and the API name the same release. To send traffic to the newest
revision again:

    gcloud run services update-traffic ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --to-latest

### 8.4 Disable

To switch off the live form, clear the URL; the page then says the API is unavailable and the
static report is unchanged:

    ./.venv-ml/bin/python ml/tools/set_manifest_api_url.py --release-id "$RELEASE_ID" --clear
    git add docs/data/ml/manifest.json
    git commit -F - <<MSG
    ml(c): disable the live Lab form for release $RELEASE_ID

    api_url cleared in docs/data/ml/manifest.json. The page now shows the "API unavailable" note
    and the static report is unaffected. The Cloud Run service itself is untouched by this commit;
    the rest of section 8.4 covers removing public access and deleting it.

    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
    Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01T2qeexjws3qbeDg1WLsLab
    MSG
    git push origin main

To stop public access to the service, and to delete it:

    gcloud run services remove-iam-policy-binding ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --member=allUsers --role=roles/run.invoker
    gcloud run services delete ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --quiet

### 8.5 Cost controls

Zero minimum instances means no idle instance cost, and cold starts are visible on the page. Two
maximum instances and concurrency 8 bound capacity; the per-client token bucket bounds each
visitor. Budget alerts and maximum instances are not hard spending caps; the disable steps above are.
```

- [ ] **Step 16: Rehearse a rollback before anyone relies on the service**

Run:
```bash
gcloud run services update ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --update-env-vars UCC_ML_ROLLBACK_REHEARSAL=1 --quiet
gcloud run revisions list --service ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --sort-by='~metadata.creationTimestamp' --format='value(metadata.name)' --limit 2
PREVIOUS=$(gcloud run revisions list --service ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --sort-by='~metadata.creationTimestamp' --format='value(metadata.name)' --limit 2 | tail -n 1)
gcloud run services update-traffic ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --to-revisions "$PREVIOUS=100"
gcloud run services describe ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --format='value(status.traffic)'
curl -sS "$(gcloud run services describe ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --format='value(status.url)')/readyz"; echo
gcloud run services update-traffic ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --to-latest
gcloud run services describe ucc-ml-api --region us-central1 --format='value(status.traffic)'
```
Expected: the update creates revision `…-00002-…`; the list prints `…00002…` then `…00001…`; after the first traffic change the traffic shows `percent: 100` on `$PREVIOUS` (`…00001…`), and `/readyz` still reports the same release id; after `--to-latest` the traffic shows `latestRevision: True` with `percent: 100`. The extra environment variable is inert: the API never reads it.

```bash
import json
import urllib.request

manifest = json.load(open("docs/data/ml/manifest.json", encoding="utf-8"))
release = next(r for r in manifest["releases"] if r["release_id"] == manifest["current"])
metrics = json.load(open("docs/data/ml/" + release["metrics_url"], encoding="utf-8"))
with urllib.request.urlopen(release["api_url"] + "/v1/model", timeout=60) as response:
    model = json.load(response)
print("static:", manifest["current"], metrics["release_id"], "api:", model["release_id"])
assert manifest["current"] == metrics["release_id"] == model["release_id"], "release ids differ: do not publish api_url"
print("release ids match")
PY
```
Expected: `<release_id>: api_url = https://ucc-ml-api-….run.app`; `1 file changed` (only `api_url` and `generated_at` move); `static: <id> <id> api: <id>` and `release ids match`. If the ids differ, run the tool again with `--clear` and stop.

## Self-review (against contract v1, the context pack and the Codex plan)

**Contract coverage**

| Item | Where |
|---|---|
| K1 plan format: `Create` / `Replace in` / `Append to`, `Run:` + `Expected:`, `git add <paths>` + `git commit -F -`, both trailers | every task |
| K3 label vocabulary and statistics: resolved statuses only, blind repeats dropped, the K3 block published | Task 3 `resolve_labels`; Task 2 `test.labels` projection; Task 5 the five label tables |
| K4 config: `load_config` + `RunConfig.section`, and this plan never writes `ml/configs/v1.yaml` | Task 3 `export_public`; Task 4's bounds test reads `section("service")`; Task 6's resource |
| K5 artefact paths: every location from `artefact_paths(cfg)` | Tasks 3, 5, 6; `exporting.ExportPaths` and `repo_root_from` deleted |
| K6 provenance: `read_json`, `write_json`, `sha256_file`, `utc_now_iso`, `finite_or_none`, `verify_sha256sums`, `BundleIntegrityError` imported, never redefined | Tasks 1–4, 11, 12; `exporting`'s own digest, SHA256SUMS and JSON helpers deleted |
| K7 readers: `read_candidates`, `read_splits`, `read_labels` | Task 3 `export_public`; Task 6 `label_counts_by_split` |
| K8 inference: `load_release_bundle`, `CaseInput`, `Prediction`, `predict_cases`, `bundle.release_id`, `bundle.threshold`; contributions as `{block, feature, contribution}`; `score-batch` the only batch scorer | Task 1 `open_release`; Task 3 reads the predictions parquet; Task 4 the live path; the seams `load_bundle`, `to_case_input`, `predict`, `prediction_to_dict`, `normalise_contributions`, `read_release_id`, `read_threshold`, `score_pool`, `score_example_pool`, `write_batch_predictions` are all deleted |
| K9 public metrics: an explicit whitelist over the REAL release `metrics.json`; never `frozen.frozen_dir`, never `provenance` beyond commit SHAs and package versions, never a per-case row, never the VALIDATION block | Task 2 `project_public_metrics`; `test_the_projection_publishes_the_test_block_and_nothing_private`; Task 10 Step 7's grep |
| K10 synthetic world: `build_synthetic_release`, no hand-rolled bundle | Task 1's `synthetic_release_dir`; Tasks 3, 4, 5, 9 use it; `write_synthetic_bundle`, `fit_synthetic_pipeline`, `synthetic_metrics`, `baseline_for`, `synthetic_candidates/splits/labels`, `frame_for` and `FEATURE_COLUMNS` are all deleted |
| K11 CLI: `export-public` registered at Plan A's two marker lines, both re-emitted unchanged, the implementation imported inside `cmd_export_public` | Task 3 Steps 4–5 |
| K15 honesty: the release's own disclosure (pooled from `disclosure_by_round` when its rounds differ, the literal when they do not) and the K3 statistics in every public document; curated examples flagged and never a benchmark; the founder approves the named list | Tasks 2, 3; Task 5's `label-provenance` and label tables; Task 10 Step 10 |
| K16 unchanged rulings: read-only DuckDB (this plan never opens it), the separate page, static first, one map link, commit by explicit path, `225 passed` after every task | Global Constraints; every task's suite step |

**Placeholder scan.** No "TBD", no "TODO", no "similar to Task N", no prose-described edit. Every file change is `Create`, `Replace in` or `Append to` with its complete content; every command sits in a fenced `bash` block after `Run:` with an `Expected:` line. The only values not fixed at writing time are genuine runtime inputs, and each is derived by a command or typed by the founder at his gate: the release id (read from `docs/data/ml/release_v1.sha256` or the manifest), the Cloud Run URL (`gcloud run services describe`), his project id and budget amount (Tasks 11–12), and the revision names (`gcloud run revisions list`).

**Type and name consistency.** `open_release` / `OpenRelease` (Task 1) is used by Tasks 2, 3 and 4; `synthetic_release_dir` and `synthetic_world_config` (Task 1's conftest) are used by Tasks 3, 4, 5 and 9; `project_public_metrics` and `evaluation_summary` (Task 2) are used by Task 4; `contribution_rows` (Task 3) is used by Task 4; `export_public` and its CLI `export-public --config PATH [--release-dir DIR]` (Task 3) are used by Tasks 5, 6, 7 and 10; `make_preview(out_dir, *, repo_root, bundle_dir) -> Path` (Task 5) is referenced by `ml/DEPLOY.md` (Task 7); `MlConfigResource`, `ManualRunConfig`, `LabelQueueConfig`, `ReleaseConfig`, `run_cli_step` and `current_release_dir` are all defined in Task 6; `record_deploy_decision.py --require deploy` (Task 11) is Task 12 Step 1; `set_manifest_api_url.py` (Task 12) is the only writer of a live `api_url` and is named in the DEPLOY.md section Task 12 writes; the page's four path lists are the single definition both page test files read.

**What the neighbouring plans owe this one.** Plan A: the two `cli.py` marker lines, `artefact_paths` with `predictions_dir` and `public_data_dir`, the `service` and `export` config sections, and the three readers. Plan B: `RELEASE_FILES`, `compute_release_id`, `load_release_bundle`, `CaseInput`, `Prediction`, `predict_cases`, `run_score_batch`, `build_synthetic_release`, and the K9 `metrics.json` whose TEST block this plan projects. All of them exist in Plans A and B as written; Task 1's precondition command fails loudly if one is missing.

**Deliberately not in this plan.** Cloud Run latency is not published as a number: cold starts show on the page as they happen, and the local measurement of Task 3 is labelled local. There is no aggregated monitoring dashboard: Task 4 emits one structured log line per prediction, which Cloud Logging keeps. The integrated map tab is out of v1 (pack decision 6). No visitor input is stored. The model never changes `scope_all`, the headline counts or the existing map, and the one link added to the map is a link.
